# 第10讲 代词(一)——人称代词

代词(Pronoun)属于封闭词类。现代英语有一个发达的代词系统，包括人称代词(Personal Pronoun)、物主代词(Possessive Pronoun)、反身代词(Reflexive Pronoun)、相互代词(Reciprocal Pronoun)、指示代词(Demonstrative Pronoun)、疑问代词(Interrogative Pronoun)、关系代词(Relative Pronoun)和不定代词(Indefinite Pronoun)。本讲介绍人称代词。

## 10.1 人称代词概说—

人称代词主要是指人的代词。英语的人称代词有三种人称形式(Person):第一人称(First Person)指说话人自己；第二人称(Second Person)指说话的对象，即听话人；第三人称(Third Person)指谈论的对象。三种人称又各有单、复数形式；第三人称单数还有阳性、阴性、中性的区别。这样，人称代词就具有下列各种语法形式：

单数I,me,my,mine第一人称复数we,us,our,ours单数you, you,your,yours第二人称复数you,you,your,yours单数he,him,his, his(阳性) she,her,her, hers(阴性)第三人称it, it, its, its(中性)复数 they, them, their, theirs在上列人称代词的形式中包括了主格(Subjective Case)、宾格(Objective Case)和属格(Genitive Case)。这样从“格”的形式划分，英语的人称代词又可归纳为：

主格：I, you, he, she,it, we,you, they宾格：me,you,him, her, it,us,you, them属格：my,mine, your,yours, his, his, her, hers, its,our,ours, your,yours, their, theirs上述人称代词又各有自己的反身形式：myself, ourselves, yourself, yourselves, himself, herself, itself, themselves。兹综合人称代词的各种形式列表如下：

人称代词反身代词 物主限定词 物主代词④主格 宾格单数 I me myself my mine第一人称复数 we us ourselves our ours单数 you you yourself your yours第二人称复数 you you yourselves your yours阳性 he him himself his his单数 阴性 she her herself her hers第三人称 中性 it it itself its 一复数 they them themselves their theirs以上所示人称代词的各种形式用在语篇中必须注意它们与其所指代的“先行项”之间在人称、数、性、格等方面保持一致。这就是本章所要交代的问题。

## 10.2 人称一致

人称一致指代词的人称形式必须和它的先行项保持协调。这有两重意义：第一，在同一句以及上下句中，代词和它的先行项必须在人称上保持一致；第二，在语篇中，说话人或者写文章的人必须保持人称的一致，是以第一人称口气说话，还是以第三人称口气说话，必须首尾一贯。

1)句子中的人称一致在句子中，先行项的人称和单、复数决定着代词的人称形式。例如：

Jane and/may move to Oklahoma. We hear that the climate there is healthful. You and Lucy can go there by car.You don't have to walk along with us. The children next door stole a toy from my son. Their mother told them to return the toy, but they said it was theirs. Tom spoke to his mother,but she didn't hear him. Clever as she was, the ginl found not a little difficulty in solving the problem. I cannot find anything wrong with this car,it runs well and sounds smooth.如果代词的人称形式与它的先行项不一致就会造成病句。试观察：

× When one goes to see her,you should plan on spending the whole day there because she always has to bake something for you to eat.这句话的毛病在于无端地在中途改变人称。上文when-分句的主语是单数第三人称one,而随后的代词却用了第二人称。纠正的方法就是要求在人称上保持前后一致：

When you go to see her,you should...或者When one goes to see her,he/one should . 2)语篇中的人称一致人称一致也是保持语篇统一性的重要要求。例如：

A week before Christmas, my friend and I flew to Madrid and then traveled by train through Spain, France and Germany. On Christmas Eve we arrived in a small town in southern Germany①物主代词即人称代词属格，分为名词性物主代词和形容词性物主代词。本书将形容词性物主代词称作物主限定词。

88 第10讲

and were amazed to find so much activity in the town. The streets were crowded with people and the shops were full of all kinds of interesting things. We walked around for hours and then just before midnight we went to listen to Christmas songs. We returned to our hotel late that night, looking forward to the next day when we planned to have Christmas dinner at the best restaurant in town. However, in the morning the streets were deserted. To make matters worse, everything was closed uptight -even the restaurants. We searched in vain for hours and finally had to return to our hotel feeling unhappy and lonely. Our Christmas “feast”was a sack of fruit which my friend had happened to buy the day before. Our thoughts sadly tumed to home where, at that moment,our families must have been wishing us a“Merry Christmas”!又例如：

In order to see the sunset, I got everything ready before five in the afternoon. Then I went to the East Hill. At that time, the sun was already in the west but it was still shining.Its light was so bright that I couldn't even open my eyes. When arrived at the East Hill, the light became a bit yellow.After a short while, it was completely golden, then red. The sun nearly set. It was like a big red ball. The cloud around it was also painted red. Little by little the cloud covered more and more of the sun. At last it disappeared behind the cloud. It was dark and l huried back.综述以上两例：第一例是从头至尾以第一人称口气生动地描述了假日出境旅游的亲身感受；第二例是以第三人称口气细致地再现了云天变幻的落日美景。这就是通过代词的人称一致而达成的语篇统一性。如果违反了人称一致的原则，那就会有损于语篇的质量。试分析以下一段文字：

* We are allbom into this world as equals, but for various reasons, not all people are treated as equals. This inequality begins when you reach the age of five, for that is when you will enter elementary school. In school, the child is no longer "Mommy's daring."You now have to prove yourself to the other children and also to your teacher. If one seems different from the other students, they are treated differently, and these differences could be anything: pants, shoes, speech, religion, and so forth. It really doesn't matter.Yet right from the start, you think that as long as you are different, there is something wrong with you.这一段话，以第一人称开首，但随后一会儿用第二人称，一会儿又用第三人称，这就破坏了语篇的统一性。正确的处理方法应该用同一个人称贯穿到底：

We are all bon into this world as equals, but for various reasons, not all of us are treated as equals. This inequality begins when we reach the age of five, for that is when we will enter elementary school. In school, we are no longer "Mommy's darlings." We now have to prove ourselves to the other children and also to our teacher. If we seem different from the other children, we are treated differently. These differences could be anything: pants, shoes, speech, religion,and so forth. They really don't matter.Yet right from the start, we think that as long as we are different, there is something wrong with us.当然，以上这一段话还可以通篇用第二人称或者第三人称来改写。无论如何，一个语篇在人称上必须首尾一贯。这就是语篇中的人称一致。

下面做一则练习，要求处理好语篇中的人称一致问题。

### EXERCISE 10A

Rewrite the following passage, using a consistent presiding pronoun and making other necessary alterations: When it comes to making a conscious effort to help keep a public place clean, most people just don't make the effort.I'm a maintenance man for a department store.If you did make the effort to help keep the public place where I work clean, we probably wouldn't have a job. The area that you have to spend the most time cleaning is the employees'lunchroom.They go there dur-ing breaks,lunch,and dinner. The maintenance department supplies the waste containers for garbage and the ashtrays for cigarette butts. When they finish their food, they will generally either throw their papers on the floor or leave it on the table. One will on occasion throw their papers in the garbage.An employee who smokes will either flick their ashes on the floor or on their table.Everybody's butts I usually find on the floor and in half-filled soda cups. The cigarettes may be found anywhere other than in the ashtray, because you steal the ashtrays or you fill it with gum. Sometimes an employee will remark, “Aren't these people pigs? They don't even clean up after themselves,"as they proceed to walk away from their littered table.

## 10.3“数”的一致

人称代词和它的先行项还应在单、复数方面保持一致。例如：

One must remember to remain very still when one/he attends a concert. Professor Smith and I went to the States together. We stayed there for three weeks. The women in the committee expressed their opinions forcefully. I recognized one of the girls, but I didn't speak to her.还有以下几点值得注意：

1)在everyone 等复合代词之后当先行项为everyone,everybody,no one, nobody,anyone,anybody,someone,somebody 等复合词时，代词以及相应的限定词通常按照语法一致原则用单数形式。例如；Anybody can attend the meeting if he is interested. Everybody talked at the top of his voice. If anyone calls, tell him I'll be back later.但在非正式语体中，也可根据意义一致的原则，用复数代词。例如：

Someone left their umbrella behind yesterday. Would they please collect it from the office? If anybody wants to give their name for the trip to Scotland, will they please do it before lunchtime? No one could have blamed themselves for that.尤其是当先行项为everybody,everyone时，其后用复数代词还比较自然。例如：

Everyone was clapping their hands. Did everybody leave the dance early because they weren'tenjoying themselves?当先行项为everything,anything, something,nothing时，随后的代词以及相应的限定词一般只按语法一致原则用单数形式。例如：

Anything on the table can be thrown away, cant it? 2)在某些并列结构之后当先行项为某些并列结构时，一般根据该并列结构的单、复数意义来决定代词以及相应限定词的单、复数形式。例如：

90 第10讲

My friend and roommate has agreed to lend me his car. My friend and my roommate have agreed to lend me their cars. 3)在某些集体名词之后当先行项为某些集体名词时，随后的代词以及相应的限定词依该集体名词用于何种意义而定。例如：

The team has won its first game. The team are now on the floor taking their practice shots at the basket. 4)在某些“复数名词或代词+each”之后当先行项为复数名词或代词作句子主语并带有each作同位语时，如果each 出现在动词之前，随后的人称代词或相应的限定词用复数；如果each 位于动词之后，随后的人称代词或相应的限定词用单数。例如：

They each had their problems. They had each his own problem. The plans each have their advantages and disadvantages. I gave the girls each a toy she likes best.下面做一则选择练习。

### EXERCISE 10B

Select an appropriate item from the two given in brackets, noting in some cases both may be acceptable: 1. The cast is giving(its, their)best performance tonight. 2. Somebody left(his, their)raincoat on this rack;(he, they) will have to come and get it. 3. Most mystery stories(侦探小说)don't end the way you expect(it, them)to. 4. Hawaii is also known for the many beautiful islands(it has, they have). 5. Because the United States uses more oil than(it, they) can produce,(it has to, they have to)import great quantities from other countries. 6. The committee will insist on(its, their)rights. 7. The committee wishes to reconsider(its,their) decision. 8. If anybody calls, tell(him, them)I'm out. 9. Everyone thinks(he is,they are) the centre of the universe. 10. We have received your estimate for the alterations you propose but have decided that(it, they)would be too high. 11. Something strange happened, didn't(it, they)? 12. Every passenger has to carry(his, their)own luggage. 13.Everything is ready,(isn't it, aren't they)? 14. Well is one of the editors who always(takes,take) a leading role in(his,their) community. 15. On the Boy Scout hike(童子军远足)everybody was told by the scoutmaster to bring(his, their)own drinking water. 16.Neither of the women(has,have)brought(her,their)certificate. 17.Neither the members of the cabinet(内阁)nor the President will reveal(his, their)plans. 18. Either John or James, who are twins,(has,have)left(his,their)sweater. 19. No manager should do for his staff what(he, they) could do for(himself, themselves). 20.Dictatorship(独裁)is the one of many evils which (tends,tend) to perpetuate (itself, themselves).

## 10.4“性”的一致 —

现代英语的代词体系包含了“性”的标记，这是就单数第三人称代词(Personal Pronoun)、物主代词(Possessive Pronoun)、反身代词(Reflexive Pronoun)以及相应的限定词而言。当我们使用这些代词和相应的限定词时，就要注意与先行项保持“性”的一致。

1)在阳性或阴性名词之后当先行项为阳性或阴性名词时，代词以及相应的限定词分别用阳性或阴性。例如：

The boy is the brightest in the class. All the teachers like him. Mother is not quite herself today. She may have caught a cold.对于中国学生来说，要注意区别英文人名的性别。一般人名是容易区分男、女性别的。例如：

James Colins plans to open his own office in Fresno. He went there last week. Tom prides himself upon his skill as a pianist. Ruth Davis will be given considerable authority if she accepts the new position. Susan is thinking of going abroad. That idea of hers has found favour with her parents.但有些人名，如 Ally(f),Amy(f),Barney(m),Beck(f),Benny(m),Carmen(f),Cecily(f), Doyle(m),Emile(m),Frances(f),Francis(m),Giles(m),Gill(f),Hallis(m),Holly(f),Ira(m), Ina(f)等，对于中国学生来说就要注意确定其性别，随后的代词和相应的限定词才不会用错。

2)在单数通性名词之后英语中有一些通性名词(Noun of Common Gender),如 baby, child, infant, doctor, patient, lawyer,engineer, teacher, student, scholar, speaker,secretary, scientist, customer,parent, person等，既可是男性，也可是女性。当这类名词以其单数形式表示类指(Generic Reference)时，通常用阳性代词或相应的限定词来指代。例如：

An instructor should offer his students challenging projects.若要避免这种用法(比如受到女权维护者的反对时),可以改用复数形式，或者避免使用代词或限定词。

例如：

Instructors should offer their students challenging projects. An instructor should offer challenging projects to the students.在法律文书等正式语体中，还可用he or she,his or her来指代单数通性名词。例如：

An instructor should offer his orher students challenging projects.但是，除法律文书外，he or she, his or her 不宜在句中多次出现，在语篇中也不宜多次重复，否则就违反“简练”的原则。为了避免使用he or she, his or her,也可改用复数代词或者避免使用代词。例如：

Parents of a teenage child often wonder where he orshe went wrong. Parents of teenage children often wonder where they went wrong. When a customer cals, askhim orher to leave his orher phone number. When a customer calls, be sure to ask for a phone number.当先行项为 baby,child, infant等通性名词而说话人(如医生、护士)不知其性别或者不必过问其性别时，可用中性代词 it;但若说话人知其性别(比如说话人是孩子的父母)则必然要根据孩子的性别来选用代词。例如：

There's a baby in the pram. Why,it's crying. Baby is crying in the pram. She must be hungry.当通性名词表示特指含义时，随后的代词以及相应的限定词须根据上下文所表示的具体性别选用适当的形式。例如：

In her lifetime,Catherine worked day and night without thinking of her personal comfort. The doctor will forever live in the hearts of her patients. 92 第10讲

A student at Barnard College has to do her own work. (Barnard College是女子大学) 3)在单数中性名词之后当先行项为单数中性名词，即表示无生命物的名词时，代词以及相应的限定词通常用中性(it, its, itself)。例如：

That book has lost its cover. I wil put a new one onit tomorrow. Have you received my letter? It was posted a month ago. This machine works by itself.但是，如果说话人带感情色彩也可用阴性或阳性代词来指代中性物，如船员常用she称呼ship: The sailors saw a ship in the distance. She was sailing toward the rocky island.又如人们常称呼自己所喜爱的汽车为she或her: A:How's your new car? B: Terrific. She's going like a bomb.汽车的女主人也可能称汽车为he或him: The car needs some petrol.Let'sfill im upat the next filing station.如果先行项为动物名称，通常用中性代词来指代，但若说话人带有感情色彩，或者把动物拟人化，可用阳性或阴性代词。例如：

Look at that bird.It always comes to my window. The cat leaped onto my bed and coiled herself there. The blackbird rushed away to the bush, where he remained perching for about a minute.当先行项为表示天体名称或某些抽象概念的名词时，代词或相应的限定词用中性，但若说话人带感情色彩或者把这些无生命物拟人化，也可用he或she,依习惯而定。例如：

The sun is shining in all his splendid beauty. Love begins playing his old tricks every spring. History has revised her verdict. The Autumn gave golden fruit to every garden, but to the giant's garden she gave nothing.当先行项为国家名称时，如果仅视为地理名词，代词或相应的限定词用中性，但若视为政治实体或者说话人带感情色彩，代词或相应的限定词用阴性。例如：

China is in East Asia. It is one of the largest countries in the world. In the last decade of the 20th century, China began to assume her role as a world power.下面一则填空练习要求在每个空格内填入两个可供选择的代词或者相应的限定词，但有的只能有一种选择。

### EXERCISE 10C

Choose between HE(HIM,HIS,HIMSELF),SHE(HER, HERS,HERSELF) and IT(ITS,ITSELF)to fill in the blanks and supply two alternatives wherever possible: 1. After_____weighed anchor,the ship slipped out of the harbour. 2. A tall figure was moving stealthily about the room.______was the burglar. 3. Getting out of the car, he said to the man in overalls:"Fill____up,please."4. Francine was so kind to me that I shall never forget___. 5. One must not lose______temper when______is being criticized for_____conduct.

6. Once upon a time there was a wolf in sheep's clothing,hiding____among a flock of sheep. 7. The moon glittered as___rolled through the deep vault of a cloudless sky. 8. France has made it plain that______will reject the project. 9.A politician may try to be completely honest, but_______always finds____has to compromise. 10. As soon as the plane had hopped off,______picked up speed. 11. The workers put shoulders to the boat and had________launched. 12. The average homeowner has to spend______free moments struggling to keep_____private castle from decaying until it is offensive to___neighbours. 13. When bathing a baby, always hold_____securely. 14. That fox had got away four times this year, but we'll get______before_____is much older. 15. Mexico is a country in North America.________has an area of 760,373 square miles with a population of 81,140,920.(1990) 16. Mrs Lewis passes everyone in her class whether______has good grades or not. 17.My baby has got______first tooth. I8. At first the earth was large and shone in the heavens filling a great part of them, but every moment____grew smaller and more distant. I9. If a student needs advice about careers,______should consult the career officers. 20. When a person casts a ballot,________has to consider very carefully who________should vote for.

## 10.5 格的选择

人称代词有格(Case)的形态变化，这当然也涉及反身代词、疑问代词、关系代词等。英语的人称代词有三种格：主格(Subjective Case)、宾格(Objective Case)、属格(Genitive Case)。属格代词(Genitive Pronoun)又叫物主代词(Possessive Pronoun),按其语法功能分为名词性属格代词(Nominal Genitive Pronoun)和限定词性属格代词(Determinative Genitive Pronoun)。

一般说来，人称代词在句中作主语，用主格；作宾语或介词补足成分，用宾格；表示“所有”,用属格。例如：

She doesn't believe us. They are always making jokes about me. His house is much larger than mine.但是事情并非都是如此简单。关于代词格的选择，情况比较复杂，可以从以下两方面进行解说。

1)用主格还是用宾格a)人称代词在句中作主语，通常要用主格，但是，在非正式语体中，当人称代词用于不带谓语或者不带限定谓语动词的不完全句中作主语时，习惯上用宾格，而不用主格。例如：

A:Does any of you know where Tom lives? lB:Me. What! Me angry! Surely it's him with the red nose you mean, not me! What! Me(to) play him at chess?NO! Me(to)appease a chap like him? Nothing doing. b)人称代词在比较分句(Comparative Clause)中作主语，用主格；作宾语，用宾格。例如：

He isn't nearly as smart as he(is). I trust you as much asher.但在非正式语体中，人称代词在比较分句中作主语，也可用宾格。例如：

94 第10讲

She is prettier than me. You are much cleverer than her. She is as tall as him.特别是当这类人称代词带有all, both作同位语时，通常都用宾格。例如：

He is cleverer than us all. She is taller than them both. c)人称代词在句中作主语补语时，按照传统语法，该用主格。但在实际使用中习惯上多用宾格。例如：

A:Who is knocking at the door? lB:It is me. If I were her, I would take the advice. d)当人称代词在分裂句(Cleft Sentence)中作中心成分(Focal Element)时，常用主格。例如：

It is he who did it. It was she that solved the problem. It is we who must bear the burden of the tax program, even though it was they who initiated it.但在非正式语体中，作以上用法的人称代词也可用宾格。例如：

It was me that did it. It was them who saved the drowning girl.如果作以上用法的人称代词在随后的who-/ that-分句中处于宾语地位，则多用宾格。例如：

It was him that I sent to collect the money. It is her that we are talking about. e)当句子主语为“everybody/nobody+but/except+人称代词”的结构时，这种人称代词按照传统语法该用宾格，因为 but /except是介词。但在实际使用中，只要人称代词出现在主语位置上，常用主格。例如：

Nobody but she can solve our problems. Everybody except he agreed to our proposal.但若以上主语被分隔而使“but/except+人称代词”这一介词词组出现在句尾时，则人称代词用宾格：

Nobody can solve our problems but her. Everybody agreed to our proposal but him.如果“everybody / nobody + but / except+ 人称代词”结构用作句子宾语，则人称代词只能用宾格。

例如：

l interviewed everybody but him. f)在由人称疑问代词 who引导的问句或从属问句中，疑问代词的格依其在句中的地位而定：在正式语体中，作主语用主格；作宾语或介词补足成分用宾格。例如：

Who can solve our problems? Whom can we trust at such a moment in history? I don't know who can solve our problems. I don't know whom they finally selected.但在非正式语体中，人称疑问代词只要在主语位置上不论作主语或宾语都可用主格。例如：

Who did you want to nominate? Who are you talking about? I don'tknow who you want to nominate? I don't know who you are talking about?

但若人称疑问代词紧跟于介词之后，则只能用宾格。例如：

To whom did you send the parcel? From whom did you receive aletter? 9)在由关系代词 who 引导的分句中，如果作主语的who 之后跟有 I believe, I think 等插入语，在正式语体中关系代词通常用主格。例如：

Will you go and see Carlos, who l believe will be glad to help you? She is a woman who people believe may be the president of the company some day. h)在由关系代词 whoever引导的名词性分句(Nominal Clause)中，该关系代词的格也是依其在分句中的地位而定：作主语，用主格；作宾语或介词补足成分，用宾格。例如：

They always elect whoever is popular. I will not trust whomever they will elect.如果whoever是在不定式之后作补语，一般用宾格。例如：

My sympathy is with this poor man, whomever he might be thought to be. 2)用宾格还是用属格关于代词宾格和属格的选择有以下几个问题值得注意。

a)当带有人称代词作主语的-ing分词分句在主句中作宾语时，人称代词通常用属格。属格代词在本书叫物主限定词。例如：

I want to avoid his /John's thinking that I don't want to help. I don't mind their/ the girls' changing their minds.但在非正式语体中，作以上用法的人称代词也可用宾格。例如：

I want to avoid him/ John thinking that I don't want to help. I don't mind them/the girls changing their minds.当带有人称代词作主语的-ing分词分句在主句中作介词补足成分时，作此种用法的人称代词也是既可用属格也可用宾格，而以用属格居多。例如：

I told them about your/you resigning from office. lam surprised at his/him making that mistake.但是，当主句带有defer, deny,postpone等作谓语动词时，随后作宾语的-ing分词分句如果还带有人称代词作为分句主语，这种人称代词通常只用属格，不用宾格。例如：

He doesn't deny his breaking the agreement. My failure to complete the task deferred my going on a holiday.上述这类-ing分词分句如果在主句中作主语，十之八九也用属格。例如：

Her driving off so abruptly was most unfortunate. His refusing to accept the invitation is really surprising. b)当人称代词在主句中作宾语，随后又跟有-ing 分词分句作补语，这时，人称代词只能用宾格，不用属格。例如：

They caught him cheating on the exam.而不能说：

× They caught his cheating on the exam. c)当带有人称代词作主语的不定式分句在主句中作宾语时，这种作分句主语的人称代词，通常只用宾格，不用属格。例如：

Everyone wanted him to be the leader of the movement.①在非正式语体，特别是在口语中，可用宾格whom。

96 第10讲

## 10.6 人称代词的类指用法—

人称代词及其属格形式和反身形式通常指特定的人或物，这是人称代词的特指用法(Specific Reference)。但是，某些人称代词也可泛指一般的人或物。这就是人称代词的类指用法(Generic Reference)。

英语中能泛指一般“人们”的代词有四个，即one, we, you, they。one作为类指的人称代词，其意义相当于people,包括说话人和听话人在内，有属格形式ones,也有反身形式oneself。例如：

One can only do ones best. One should not overwork oneself.不定代词one通常用于正式语体。为了避免在同一句中多次重复one / ones,凡是要重复one/ ones的地方，可用he/his来取代，在美国英语中尤其如此。例如：

One must remember to remain still when he attends a concert. If one wants to see the ruins,he must find his own guide.作类指用法的we 也常见于正式语体。作者在正式书面语中常用we来表示包括作者和读者在内的一般的人，口气亲切，使读者容易接受作者的观点，产生共鸣。例如：

We must be conscientious in our work if we value our careers.作类指用法的you与不定代词 one同义，均指包括说话人和听话人在内的一般的人，但只用于非正式语体，口气亲切。例如：

If you want to see the ruins,you must go on foot.作类指用法的 they 也常见于非正式语体，但所指的人不包括说话人和听话人在内。例如：

They say it's going to rain this afternoon.要注意，除非表意上的需要，类指的人称代词通常不在句中混杂使用，例如以下情况就是不可取的：

× One must be patient if they want to succeed.× If we look into the minutes, you will be surprised how much time was wasted in useless discussion.当然，如果是表意上的需要，那么中途更换类指代词则是可以的。例如：

if you want to see the ruins, they will tell you that you must have a guide.中性单数第三人称代词it也可作类指用法，即泛指一类的物。例如：

Music is my favourite subject. Is it yours? I don't like cheese. I don't even want to taste it.中性复数第三人称代词 they 也可作类指用法。例如：

Oysters(牡蛎)are delicious, and they are inexpensive.由上述诸例可以看出，当中性第三人称代词 it和 they 作类指用法时，其先行项通常是带有零冠词的名词词组。

下面一则练习要求按照正式语体标准选用适当的代词格。

### EXERCISE 10D

Choose an appropriate case form according to standards of formal writing: 1. Just between you and(I,me), that man is crazy. 2. The petition was drafted by Nelson, Wright, Mansfield,and(I, me). 3. It was(she, her)who finally spoke up.

4. Everybody, including(I, me),failed the test. 5. Mark was very hurt and began spreading many lies about Troy and(she, her). 6. No one will be responsible for it, neither you nor(she,her). 7. To(who, whom)should I address this letter? 8. The child(who, whom) we thought was lost was found in the next block. 9. Stanley had no doubt about(who, whom)would be elected minister of defense. 10. He promised to support(whoever,whomever) of the Republican prospects(共和党候选人)was chosen. 11. I found(his,him)lying by the roadside. 12. The bush is dying:(its, its) leaves are covered with mildew(霉). 13. I wouldn't trust(anyone's else,anyone else's) judgement. 14. I cannot understand(him,his) refusing to do that for me. 15. The youth(who,whom)they said was the most advanced in the group did prove to be the leader. 16. They will arrest(whoever, whomever) is the organizer of the riot. 17. I don't mind(him,his)criticizing me so severely. 18. The family resisted the idea of(she,her) leaving home. 19. The woman(who,whom)we thought to be(she,her) turned out to be her sister. 20. It is difficult to predict(who, whom) the electorate(选民)will vote for. 98 第10讲

# 第11讲 代词(二)——物主代词、反身代词、 指示代词、相互代词、 不定代词

本讲介绍物主代词、反身代词、指示代词、相互代词、不定代词以及相应的限定词。

## 11.1 物主代词和物主限定词

1)物主代词如前所述，本书所讲的物主代词仅指名词性属格代词，包括 mine, yours, his, hers,ours, yours, theirs,列表如下：

人称 单数 复数第一人称 mine ours第二人称 yours yours阳性 his第三人称 theirs阴性 hers由上表可见，单数第三人称阳性物主代词his 与相应的物主限定词同形；单数第三人称中性代词的属格形式its通常只用作物主限定词，而无名词性属格形式。

物主代词在句中起名词词组的作用，可用作主语、主语补语、宾语、介词补足成分以及同位语等。

例如：

The car in the lot is his. Mine is the one with a broken light. Theirs is the only house here that is being painted. You have your ticket; we lost ours. I'll do my work and you yours. His car is just like hers. You will find your books among mine on the shelf. Someone hit a car in the parking lot,mine unfortunately.

物主代词还可在“双重属格”(Double Genitive)结构中作介词of的补足成分。例如：

He is a friend of mine. We were absorbed in that song of yours. 2)物主限定词物主限定词(Possessive Determiner)包括my,your, his, her, its, our, their,my own,your own, his own等，它们能与单、复数和不可数名词搭配，表示多种意义。

a)表示“所有”关系“所有”关系是物主限定词和名词中心词之间最主要的语义关系。例如：

Half his money was gone. The word has found its way into the dictionaries. Our youngest child is only six. Their main export is sugar.物主限定词可以和own连用，表示对比和强调。例如：

Mind yourown business(别管闲事)! He will stick to his own opinion(坚持己见). She can't read herown handwriting(自己写的字，自己也看不清).物主限定词+own还可用于of 之后，形成of one's own的结构，更加强调特定的所有关系。例如：

She wanted to have a house of herown. He always has views of his own. This word has a distinct meaning of its own. b)表示主一动关系就是说，物主限定词与名词中心词的关系是一种“主语+谓语动词”的关系。例如：

Yourunexpected amival surprised us all. His criticism was not quite to the point.在这里，your unexpected arrival相当于you arrived unexpectedly; his criticism相当于he criticized。

c)表示动一宾关系与物主限定词相搭配的名词中心词，如果是由及物动词转化而来，那么在一定上下文中，物主限定词与名词中心词的语义关系也可能是一种动一宾关系，即物主限定词在含义上可能成为动作的承受者。例如：

When Tom died, Marie grieved for his loss. We knew the intention of the speaker and can watch its transmission(播送)to the listener.上述第一句相当于... Marie grieved for losing him;第二句相当于...transmitting it to the listener。

d)表示来源物主限定词在某些搭配中还可以表示来源。例如：

Lu Hsun is my favourite author.His works are always a source of inspiration to me. I received her letter this moning. Allnis lectures were boring.在上述第一句中，his works=works by Lu Hsun;在第二句中，her letter=a letter from her;在第三句中，all his lectures=all the lectures given by him。

物主限定词的上述诸种用法与名词属格的部分用法是基本一致的。

e)用在某些成语中在某些成语中，物主限定词和名词中心词之间的语义关系不很明确，只能作为固定搭配从整体上去理解。例如：

The following item from THE NEW YORKER magazine is reprinted inits entirety(全文转载). 100 第11讲

The wind was blowing its hardest(风刮得很紧). He said he had had his fll of travel in foreign countries(已经作了够多的国外旅行). Eat and drink your fil(尽管吃饱喝足)!

## 11.2 反身代词

人称代词都有自己的反身形式(Reflexive Form),这叫做反身代词(Reflexive Pronoun)。它们是myself,ourselves,yourself,yourselves, himself, herself, itself, themselves。此外，不定人称代词one 也有反身形式 oneself。因此，反身代词共有9个。这9个反身代词在人称、性、数方面与一般的人称代词是一致的。所不同的是，反身代词没有格的形式。兹将反身代词的各种形式列表如下：

人称 单数 复数第一人称 myself ourselves第二人称 yourself yourselves第三人称 himself(阳性) themselves herself(阴性) itself(中性) oneself(通性)反身代词可在句中作直接宾语、间接宾语、主语补语，也可作名词词组或代词的同位语以及介词补足成分。反身代词的这些用法归纳起来，可分为两大类：一类是强调性用法(Emphatic Use);另一类是非强调性用法(Unemphatic Use)。

1)反身代词作同位语反身代词作同位语属于强调性用法，这时，反身代词要重读；如果反身代词是强调主语，其位置比较灵活。例如：

The manager himseff will interview Lucy. The manager will interview Lucy himself. Nancy herself will perform the operation. Nancy will perform the operation herself.有时为了特别强调，反身代词还可出现在主语之前。例如：

Myself, I can't come, but I'll ask someone to help you. Themselves, the ministers will go and see him.当反身代词不是强调主语，而是强调句子其他成分时，其位置只能直接跟在被强调的成分之后。例如：

I spoke to the president himself. I like the diamond itself but not the setting.用反身代词作同位语，要注意避免某些歧义现象。例如：

?Mrs Smith will go and see Nancy herself.? He spoke to the president himself. 2)反身代词作介词补足成分反身代词作介词补足成分一般属于非强调性用法，不需重读。例如：

I have nothing to say for myself. You think too much of yourself. He's not womed about himself.但若表示对比意义，便需重读，从而属于强调性用法。例如：

He thinks of himself but not of you.

She was worried about herself, not about him.反身代词与某些介词搭配可表示特殊意义，这也属于强调性用法。例如：

The old man lives byhimself(独居). She did the work(all) byherseff(独立工作). This machine works by itself(自动运转). The enemy will not perish of himself(自行灭亡). This matter is between ourselves(勿为外人知道). If you don't believe me, you may go and see for yourself(亲自去看). Howl wish to have a room to myself(独有一室). He was beside himself with joy(欣喜若狂). 3)反身代词作宾语反身代词作宾语通常属于非强调性用法，不需重读，常见于某些“动词+反身代词+副词”的固定搭配中。例如：

You mustpullyourself together(振作起来). She tookherself away(走开). He often puts himself fonward(摆架子), Carl played a joke on Bob and gave himself away(露马脚)by laughing. He has really put himselfout(作出努力)to see that everybody is comfortable.也常见于某些“动词+反身代词+介词”的固定搭配中。例如：

'd like to avail myselfof(利用)this opportunity to make a request to you. She always prides herself on(夸耀) having a glorious past. He absented himselfrom(没有出席)the meeting. Very soon we adapted ourselves to(适应了)the hot weather. He applied himself to(专心致志于)the task. You should not surrenderyourself to despair(陷于绝望),还常见于某些成语中。例如：

He made anuisance of himself(令人讨厌). I don't want to make a show of myself(出丑)before strangers. Don't make a pig of yourself(狼吞虎咽). He could have hugged himself forjoy(沾沾自喜)at his success. She should have kicked herself(严厉自责)for committing such a mistake. For a long time she had kept herself to herself(不与他人往来).在某些SVO结构中，反身代词常与behave,conduct, deport等动词搭配表示举止行为的好坏。

例如：

He behaved himself fairly well(举止端庄)when he was at college. She always conducts herself like a lady(举止大方). The boys were trained to deport themselves like gentlemen(作风正派), 4)反身代词的其他用法问题反身代词作主语补语属于强调性用法，这种用法通常表示身体状况或作并列补语的一部分。例如：

Frank is not quite himself today(身体不适). The girl will beherself in no time(很快康复). Now l feel quite myself(感觉好多了). Ah, that's it.You're yourself again(恢复正常了). The other two are Mr Stein and myself. 102 第11讲

在正式语体中，通常不用反身代词单独作主语或者用反身代词作并列主语的一部分。例如：

Herself is the cause of the difficulty. Pat and myself are writing a novel.按照正式语体的标准，这两句都被视为病句，应改为：

She/She herself is the cause of the difficulty. Pat andI/1myself are writing a novel.但在非正式语体中，用反身代词作并列主语的一部分是比较常见的现象，因此，以上第二句Pat and myself are writing a novel,在非正式语体中是很普通的。

下面做一则改错练习。

### EXERCISE 11A

Correct errors, if any, in the following sentences: 1. The furniture is their's, but the house is our's. 2. The dog is lying on its back, but I can't tell whether its dead or not. 3. I've bought several paintings of him. 4. The professor interviewed the applicant himself. 5. She has nothing to say for her. 6. We were beside us with joy. 7. He always conducts him like a scholar. The sky has rained it out. Jane and himself collaborated on a new novel. 10. No one but myself really understands him. No one but herself knows Russian. 12. I saw his waving a flag in the crowd. The reward was divided among us three, George, Tom,and I. You probably know to who I am referring. Could it have been him whom was injured? We agreed to accept whomever they thought was the best foreman. O fall we men whom I think should be available for ofice, Thompson is the first who comes to mind. I cannot tolerate such men as he. They deferred him going home on furlough until next month. Settle the question with whomever wrote the report. Johnson's father approved of he staying in China for another year. I don't like you to arrive late. They will pay whoever they find the artist to be. The girls played their instruments.Them performing for us was a nice surprise. He is the one whom I believe is interested in entomology(昆虫学)。

The sergeant asked whomever did it to step forward. They declared the winners to be Jane,Joe and I. It looks as if no one except himself can be there early. If one doesn't want to get lost in the mountains,you must have a guide. You never know whether we're going to qualify for this test or not.You see the rules are changed every day.

## 11.3 指示代词和指示限定词—

指示代词(Demonstrative Pronoun)this, that, these,those与指示限定词(Demonstrative Determiner)同形，关于这两类词的用法可以放在一起阐述。另外，还有一个 such,当它用作代词和限定词时类似指代词，因此也放在本节一起介绍。

1)指人还是指物指示代词this/that, these/those通常指物，不指人。例如：

This had resulted in a score or more deaths(导致二十多人的死亡). This chair is more comfortable than that. Things such as these are the best themes(主题)for poets. Of all the pictures l like those best.但是，指示限定词的用法比较灵活，它们既可指物，也可指人。试比较：

* Is she going to marry that?√Is she going to marry that man?* Come and meet these over here.√Come and meet these people over here.当指示代词单独用作某些SVC结构的主语时，它们才既可指物，也可指人，比如当说话人在介绍他人时，可以说：

This is my sister. That is her husband.或者指着一张照片或名单说：

These are my parents. Those are my children. Are these the students who have registered?在电话中，人们可以说：

This is Johnny. Is that Susan?总之，用指示代词指人是受到严格限制的。

2)指近还是指远对于说话人来说，指示对象距离的远近也关系到指示词(Demonstrative)的选择问题。一般说来，指近用this/these;指远用that/those。例如：

This is my friend Charlie Brown. That is David my roommate(pointing out someone in a crowd). Here we are. This is our hotel, isn't it? We're now in Oxford Circus, half-way along Oxford Street, and that street over there is Regent Street,famous all over the world for its splendid shops. These cabins are for the second-class passengers, and those on the top floor are for the first-class passengers.在时间观念上，也有远近之分：一般说来，this/these常用以表示现在或不久将来的事态，而that/those则常用以表示过去的事态。例如：

Suddenly he remembered that time in Rome, a year after they were maried; those June nights and the fireflies. How very nice to see you again after all these years! Do you still remember the days when the city was under Japanese occupation. In those days, people scarcely had enough to eat. 104第11讲

3)后照应还是前照应就指示代词/指示限定词和它们的照应对象的相互位置来说，指示照应通常是后照应，即指示词的照应对象通常出现在上文。例如：

It will be a long time before any landing on Mars(登上火星)can be attempted. This will only be possible when scientists have least a lot more about the atmosphere that surrounds the planet (火星周围的大气层). Suddenly, I saw the Oficer's face light up(喜气洋洋).He had spotted a tiny bottle(发现一只小瓶子) at the bottom of my case and he pounced(一把抓住)on it with delight. "Perfume(香水),eh?"he asked sarcastically(讥讽地),“You should have declared that(这玩意儿该报税吧),”The platforms extend to a depth of over 100 feet under the sea. These alone took sixteen months to build(单单建造这些平台就花了一年零四个月的时间). Boxing matches were very popular in England two hundred years ago. In those years, boxers fought with bare fists for prize money(当年拳师们为了争得奖金光着拳头进行角斗).在上述四例中，this指上文 any landing on Mars; that指上文 perfume; these 指上文 the platforms; in those years指上文 two hundred years ago。this/that 除能指代上文中出现的名词词组外，还可把上文中整个分句、整个句子甚至若干个句子所表达的意思作为自己的照应对象，这叫做“广阔照应”(Broad Reference),意思是照应对象不限于一个名词词组。例如：

At first glance, the desert seems completely barren of animal life, but this is an illusion.(乍看起来，这块不毛之地完全没有动物，但这是一种错觉。) The beginning of the book is more interesting than the conclusion. That's why I'm not very keen on the book.(这本书开头比结尾较为有趣，因此我不很喜爱这本书。) She suddenly got into a panic and stopped her car.This made the driver following her brake hard. (她突然惊慌起来，把车刹住。这就使紧随其后的司机猛烈刹车。)在上述第一例中 this的照应对象是上文整个分句 the desert seems completely barren of animal life;在第二、三例中 that和this分别指上文整句的意思。又例如；Modem sculpture(现代雕刻)rarely surprises us any more. The idea that modem art(现代艺术) can only be seen in museums is mistaken. Even people who take no interest in art cannot have failed to notice(不会不看见)examples of modern sculpture on display(在展览中)in public places. Strange forms stand in gardens, and outside buildings and shops. We have got quite used to(对⋯⋯·习以为常)them. Some so-called “modem” pieces have been on display for nearly fifty years. In spite of this, some people— including myself— were surprised by a recent exhibition of modem sculpture...·⋯⋯尽管如此，有些人—包括我自己在内—对最近举行的一次现代雕刻展览感到诧异⋯⋯)在这里，in spite of this所指的内容实际上概括了上文由六个完全句构成的整个语段的内容。由此可见，指示代词 this所照应的领域往往是很广阔的。

在前、后照应问题上，this/that和 these/those 在用法上还有所区别。一般说来，this/these既可作后照应，也可作前照应，而that/those通常只作后照应，不作前照应。

当 this/these作前照应时，它的照应对象通常是下文中的分句或句子，甚至是下文中几个句子所表达的内容。例如：

This is what 1mean: You should have changed,not deleted(删除),that paragraph. I said this. There should be no error, no delay and no waste.(我说过，不应有错误、拖延或浪费。) This I can tell you:if you don't want money but are content to earn just enough to keep body and soul together(勉强度日),then go. This should interest you, if you're still keen on boxing.The world heavyweight championship(重

量级拳击锦标赛)is going to be held in Chicago(芝加哥)next June, so you should be able to watch it live(观看实况). The guide was very much interested in city planning(城市规划).At one point in the tour she told the passengers about some of the plans that were being made to improve the city. These are some of the things that must be done:(1)New hospitals must be built.(2) More parking space(停车场地)must be provided.(3) The airports must be expanded.(4) New schools must be built. (5)Public transportation(公交运输)must be improved.在上述前四例中，this均指下文的一个分句或一个句子；而第五例中的 these则指下文的五个句子。

that/those很少用于前照应，偶尔见之，也带有讽刺意味，例如：

I like that! He damaged my car and then blamed me for letting him use it.(这倒有趣!他损坏了我的车子，反而怪我借车给他。) How do you like that? He stabs you in the back and then professes to be your friend.(你说我会喜欢吗?他暗箭伤人，回过头来又说是你的朋友。)

## 11.4 相互代词

相互代词(Reciprocal Pronoun)只有两个词项each other和one another。例如：

Bob and Tom speak highly of each other. They two like each other and help each other. We gave presents to one another. The accomplices(共犯)are accusing one another.传统语法书规定 each other 指两个人或物之间的相互关系，而one another 则指两个以上人或物之间的相互关系。其实，这条“规定”在实际使用中往往并不严格遵守，两者常可交替使用，只是each other 多用于非正式语体，而one another则多用于正式语体：

They sat for two hours without talking to each other/one another. Many a newlywed couple(新婚夫妇)has received the admonition:"Be kind to one another!"

## 11.5 不定代词和不定限定词

英语的不定代词(Indefinite Pronoun)分为两大类：第一大类是数词(Numeral)和量词(Quantifier);第二大类是复合代词(Compound Pronoun): somebody anybody everybody nobody someone anyone everyone no one, none something anything everything nothing本节只交待上述第一大类的不定代词和不定限定词。

第一大类的不定代词，除个别外，一般都有两重性：当它们单独用于句中时，是代词；而当它们位于名词之前时，则是限定词。例如：

Six of them were drowned; the other six disappeared. In the house live six blind men. How much money will be needed for the project?—One milion will be enough.-One million dollars will be enough. The small town has a population of five thousand. The town has only five thousand inhabitants. I have had enough;I don't want any more. 106 第11讲

I'd like some more chips.下面对若干语义相关的量词用法作一些比较说明。

1)many, much, a lot of, lots of,plenty of等表示“多”的意思，可用 many,much, a lot of /lots of, plenty of,a load of /loads of,a heap of /heaps of, scads of, a good /great many,a large amount of等。作为限定词，many和a good/great many 之后须跟复数名词；much和a large amount of 之后须跟不可数名词。例如：

Many animals have diseases. A rocket has many parts. Much information is now transmitted through e-mail. There is much coal left in the mine. We have agreat many questions to discuss at the meeting. We are going to spend alarge amount of money on children's education. many, much,a great many等既可用作限定词，也可用作不定代词。例如：

Have you done all these exercises?—No, I haven't done very many. Have you done much work today?—No, I haven't done much. Has she read any English novels in the original?—Yes,agreat many. Has he spent much money on the house?—Oh,alarge amount. many, much 可以带有how/ too/so/as..as等修饰语。例如：

I want to know how much money will be spent on the project. How many copies do you need for your class? I have(far) too many books on the shelf. We've had too much rain in the spring. He took so many boxes with him that he had to call a taxi. It's really surprising that the boy should have so much strength at his age. You can take as many copies as you need. They can spend as much money as they need on the project.在非正式语体中，尤其是在口语中，当many/much 既不带上述的修饰语，又非处于主语的位置时，通常用于否定句和疑问句，若要表示肯定意义，通常用a lot of,lots of,或plenty of。比较：

He hasn't got much money, but he has plentyof friends. Has she got many friends here?一No,not many.一Yes, quite a lot/quite a few.一Yes, she hasalot of/lots of friends here. Are there many animals in the park?一No,not many. Did he spend much money on his pet dog?—No,not much.由上述诸例可以看出，相应于 a lot of /lots of/plenty of的名词性结构便是a lot,plenty。例如：

We have lot(of things) to do today. We have paid him plenty(of money).正因为a lot of, lots of, plenty of通常表示肯定意义，所以在某些表示不肯定口气的话语中，倾向于用many /much。例如：

I doubt whether / if there'll be many people at the show on this rainy day. I wonder whether/ if he has much information on this subject.以下表示“许许多多”的词语既可用于可数名词，也可用于不可数名词：

Loads of big red apples were on the ground. Loads of milk was distributed to the refugees. There are heaps of books on this subject. enjoyed the performance very much:there was heaps of fun. There were scads of opportunities for all of us. Scads of money has been spent on this project. 2)(a) few,(a) little表示“少”的意思，可用(a) few,(a) little,既可用作限定词，也可用作不定代词。a few,a little表示“少量”,带有肯定含义。例如：

Let's invite a few friends to come with us. Here are a few more books on this subject. There are only avery few left. I'm keeping the few that remain for tomorrow. I had alile difficulty in solving the problem. Give mea little of that wine. I'm trying to use the little French I have just learnt. few/little 若不与a连用则表示否定意义，相当于not many/much,not enough。例如：

I have very few(chocolates) left. Few(children) were tired. I have very Iittle(money) left. understood little of his speech.要注意，quite a few,a good few,not a few不表示“少”,而表示“相当多”的含义，相当于a fair number(of)。例如：

Quite a few of us are getting worried. You'll have to wait a good few weeks. little的比较级和最高级是less和least,通常只能与不可数名词搭配，但在当代英语中也有用less与复数名词搭配的。例如：

If only there were less holes in the roof.但这只见于非正式语体；在正式语体中仍以用fewer为好。

3) some,any要表示“一些”的意思，可用some,any。some是肯定词(Assertive Word),常用于肯定句；any是非肯定词(Non-assertive Word),常用于否定句或疑问句。例如：

There are some letters for me. There aren'tany letters for me. Are there any letters for me? seldom get any sleep these days. any 也常用于条件分句以及带有否定含义的句子中：

If you have any trouble, please let me know. I forgot to ask for any change(零钱).当说话人期待肯定回答时，some也可用于疑问句，比如当说话人期待来信时，他可以问道：

Are there some letters for me?当购物时向售货员提问或者主人向客人表示款待时，也可在疑问句中用some: Could I have some of these apples? Would you like some chocolate cake?当some与单数名词搭配时，some相当于a certain(“某一”)的含义；而any与单数名词搭配，则相当于every(“任何一个”)的含义。例如：

Some boy has broken a window. Any child could answer that question. 108 第11讲

4) all, both, every,each, either,neither,any这一类词，除every只能作限定词外，都是既可作限定词，也可作不定代词。例如：

all(of) the boys both (of) the boys every boy/every one of the boys each boy/each (one) of the boys either boy/either(one) of the(two)boys neither boy/neither (one) of the(two) boys any boy/any (one) of the(three or more) boys由上述诸例可以看出，这一类的限定词和不定代词在用法上有以下值得注意之处：

第一，表示“全体”,可用all和both,但all 表示三个或三个以上人或物的“全体”,而both 则表示两个人或物的“全体”。例如：

All the four applicants are below the average. All these last few days the farmers have been busy fighting drought(抗早). Both his parents are against his going there alone. The man was blind in both eyes.如果要表示“全体都不”的意思，当“全体”为三个或更多的人或物时，通常用none。例如：

None of the students failed the examination. I'l have none of your stupid ideas.(我不能接受你的那些糊涂观念。)在上述第一例中，既可用none,也可用no one: No one failed the examination.但no one只能指人，不能指物。

如果要表示两个人或物“都不”,通常要用neither: Neither student/Neither(one) of the(two) students failed the examination. Neither car/Neither(one) of the(two) cars was made in Japan.第二，表示全体中的“每个”,如果这个“全体”包含三个或更多的人或物，通常用every。例如：

Every student in the class took part in the performance. The police were closely watching his every action.如果这个“全体”包含两个或两个以上的人或物，便可以用each。例如：

Each side of the street was crowded with people.在这里，不可以用every。如果说“广场的每一边都挤满了人”,那就既可用each,也可用every: Each/Every side of the square was crowded with people. every与each的区别还在于every指许多人或物中的“每个”,侧重在全体，近乎all的含义。例如：

Every student failed the examination.=All the students failed the examination. We want every child to succeed.=We want all the children to succeed.而each则指许多人或物中的“各个”,侧重在各别。例如：

Each child will find his own personal road to success. The Queen shook hands with each player in turn after the game.第三，表示全体中的“任何一个”,也要看这个“全体”是包含三个或更多，还是只包含两个。当“全体”包含三个或三个以上，要表示其中任何一个须用any。例如：

Any(=Every) child would know that. Ask any man you meet. His gift was unknown to any (of them)except himself. These are all free: take any(of them)you like.

当“全体”只包含两个时，要表示其中任何一个须用either。例如：

There are two fights for Beijing in the moming.You can take either(one). We have two copies left. Keep either(copy)for the file.但在on either side, on either end等固定词组中有时可以兼指两个。例如：

There are warehouses on either side of the river(=on both sides of the river). He got off the train with a bundle on either arm. There are stairways at either end of the corridor.要注意，all/every ...not 结构远不如 not all/every 结构普通，因为前者在书面语中容易引出歧义。

例如：

Al/ the soldiers didn't know how to fight.这个句子既可理解为“所有的士兵都不会打仗”,相当于：

None of the soldiers knew how to fight.也可理解为“并非所有的士兵都会打仗”,相当于：

Not all the soldiers knew how to fight.因此，在书面语中，要表示“所有的人或物都不/都没有⋯⋯”或者要表示“并非所有的人或物都⋯⋯"”,最好还是用none of...和not all/every..结构为宜。

all/every...not结构，虽在日常英语中容易引出歧义，但在一些谚语中却不存在歧义问题，只能作一种解释。例如：

Al that gitters is not gold.(=Not all that glitters is gold.)(闪烁发光的未必都是黄金。) All are not friends that speak us fair.(尽说好话的未必都是朋友。) All are not hunters that blow the horn.(吹号角的未必都是猎人。意谓“高谈阔论者未必真有学问。”) Allis not butter that comes from the cow.(出身高贵者未必都是精英。) All bread is not baked in one oven.(各有所长，不能强求一致。) Every couple is not a pair.(两个人在一起未必就是夫妻。意谓“有情者未必都成眷属。”) Every day is not Sunday.(并非每一天都是星期日。含义是：不能游手好闲，无所事事。) Every shoe fits not every foot.(不可用一种尺度衡量一切。) Everyone's faults are not written in their foreheads.(不可以貌取人/对人要深入了解。)下面做一则练习，要求用表示数量概念的适当限定词或相应的不定代词填空。

### EXERCISE 11B

Fill in the blanks with appropriate determiners or corresponding indefinite pronouns semantically related to quantity: 1. A:Do you need more milk? B: No, there is too______already. 2. A:Should we get some tomatoes? B: Yes, but not as_____as that. 3. He seldom uses_____paper to start a fire. 4. I can't open this lock. There must be_____key that will open it. 5. She wanted____stamps, but there aren't______in the machine. 6. This medicine is on sale everywhere.You can get it at_______chemist's. 7.____people will believe_____story you tell them. 110 第11讲

8. We don't have to buy any more egs. There are a great_______left in the fridge. 9. Quite a______girl students major in English. 10. Bob is rather selfish. He has________thought about other people's interests. 11. He gained more marks for English.There were_____mistakes in his composition. 12. A truck collided with a car.The car was totally damaged, but_______damage was done to the truck. 13. On_____side of the street there were assembled a lot of soldiers. 14. A:Which of these two novels would you like? B:I don't like______of them.I am interested in_____one. 15. There are two Germans in his class._____major in economics. 16. There were hundreds of people at the show._____spoke highly of the exhibition. 17. All the staff members were requested to do something for the organized charities(慈善事业),but______contributed to the fund. 18. When the ship was sunk, all the sailors were drowned,______one of them. 19. The president got down from the plane and shook hands with_____one waiting at the airport. 20.A:Which of all these would you like? B:I wouldn't like_____of them.They____look too old.

## 11.6 代词照应

如前所述，代词的使用必有其所指对象(Referent)又叫先行项(Antecedent)。代词和它的所指对象之间的关系就是本节所要讲的代词照应(Pronoun Reference)。

1)代词照应的类型代词的指代对象通常是在上下文中表示出来。就代词及其指代对象(即先行项)的相互位置来说，代词照应分为后照应(Anaphoric Reference)和前照应(Cataphoric Reference)。anaphoric意为pointing backward,当指代对象出现在代词之前，从而代词必须回过头来指向它的指代对象时，这便是后照应(也可译作“回指照应”)。例如：

My father is a doctor, and he wants me to follow the same profession. Because Ann had never spoken before an audience,she was nervous. After all the crowd had contributed,Stanley thanked them. She has quit smoking because she thinks it harmful. cataphoric 意为pointing forward,如果指代对象出现在代词之后，从而代词必须向前指向它的指代对象时，这便是前照应。例如：

Ashe himselfis a doctor,my father wants me to choose medicine for a profession. Unless she works harder,Rose won't be able to pass the exams.如果代词的指代对象不是出现在上下文，而是由话语情景提供，这便是情景照应(Situational Reference)。例如：

(Husband speaking to wife, pointing to the children's bedroom)Are they asleep? (Someone wanting to meet the lady of the house) Is she in? I don't think you've met each other before. Let me introduce you. This is my niece, Miss Smith. She is my friend, Mrs White. 2)代词照应的指导原则正确使用代词有两条指导原则：一是在使用代词时通常要把它的指代对象即先行项(Referent/Antecedent)表示出来，否则代词的意义不明，甚至会引起句子语义混乱。试观察：

(Faulty) Since his mother is an accountant, he is going to major in it.在这里，代词 it没有指代对象，订正方法有二：一是给 it提供适当的先行项；二是避免使用代词：

(Revised) Since his mother is good at accountancy, he is going to major in it. (Revised) Since his mother is an accountant,he is going to major in accountancy. (Faulty)Dr Laura Casey has spent 12 years in these areas of Africa and knows what their customs are.这句话的问题在于物主限定词 their没有适合的所指对象。乍一看，似乎 these areas of Africa是their的先行项，其实不是，因为areas不会有“风俗”。这个句子经过订正应该是：

(Revised)Dr Laura Casey has spent 12 years in these areas of Africa and knows the customs of the native people there. (Faulty) He was opposed to gun control because he felt that every family should keep o nein case robbers should stage a surprise attack.这句话的问题出在替代词one上，它的先行项应该是 a gun 或guns,可是上文并没有这类词语，上文出现的gun仅是 control的前置修饰语，本身不是一个名词词组，不可以用one 来替代。订正的方法还是要给one提供一个先行项，或避免使用代词：

(Revised) He was opposed to control of guns because he felt that every family should keep one in case robbers should stage a surprise attack. (Revised) He was opposed to gun control because he felt that every family should keep a gun in case robbers should stage a surprise attack. (Faulty) put the frozen strawberries in the sink because it was leaking.这句话的毛病在于代词it 指向一个错误的对象(the sink),以致引起逻辑上的混乱。很难解释把冷冻草莓放在水斗里是因为水斗漏水。由此可知，it的真正指代对象并没有表示出来。因此，这个句子经过订正应该是：

(Revised) I put the frozen strawberries in the sink because the package was leaking. (Revised) I put the package of frozen strawberries in the sink because it was leaking.总而言之，用代词就得把它的指代对象(即先行项)表示出来，否则就得避免使用代词。

代词照应的第二条指导原则是一个代词只能有一个指代对象。如果一个代词有两个或两个以上可能的指代对象，就会引起歧义。在发生歧义的情况下，就得设法消除多余的先行项。例如：

(Faulty) When Dad was speaking to the lawyer,he was extremely ill at ease. (Revised) When he was speaking to the lawyer, Dad was extremely ill at ease. (Faulty) Ann passed Jan on the way to cafeteria, but she wouldn't even look at her. (Revised) When she passed Jan on the way to cafeteria, Ann wouldn't even look at her. (Faulty) Two weeks after Ed's uncle moved to Florida,he sold the laundromat. (Revised) Two weeks after his uncle moved to Florida, Ed sold the laundromat. (Faulty) Cabbage contains this vitamin,and it is essential to sound teeth. (Revised) Cabbage contains this vitamin, which is essential to sound teeth. (Faulty) Mother asked Mrs Spry if she could visit her before she left for Europe. (Revised) Before she left for Europe,Mother asked Mrs Spry to visit her. (Faulty) Tom sent for Pollard and told him that when Edwards was added to the staff he would serve as chief agent. (Revised) Tom sent for Pollard and told him that Edwards, when added to the staff, would serve as chief agent. (Faulty) The plane was grounded by a strike, which is what we had expected all along. (Revised) As we had expected, the plane was grounded by a strike. (Faulty) There are many children living in slum and poor areas with great potentials, but they 112 第11讲

are not enhanced, because they feel they can't get ahead. (Revised) There are many children living in slum and poor areas with great potentials, which are not enhanced, because they feel they can't get ahead.总而言之，使用代词的时候，必须保证做到一个代词只有一个指代对象，以便避免歧义。

3)人称照应就所用代词的不同种类来说，代词照应又可分为人称照应(Personal Reference)和指示照应(Demonstrative Reference)。

人称照应就是人称代词、物主代词、反身代词以及物主限定词(Possessive Determiner)与其指代对象之间的照应关系。人称照应通常是后照应，即指代对象通常出现在前，人称代词出现在后。这种照应关系可以存在于同一句中，也可以存在于前后句中。上一节中所举诸例均属此类。

人称照应也可以是前照应，即代词在上文，指代对象在下文，但是，前照应人称代词在使用上所受限制较大，不如后照应人称代词那样普通。一般说来，前照应人称代词通常出现在从属结构中，而且凡是能用前照应的场合，也可用后照应来表达。例如：

After she weighed anchor(起锚),the ship sailed out of the harbour.在这里，指代对象 the ship 出现在下文，人称代词she出现在上文的状语分句(从属结构)中，从而构成前照应关系。这种句子也可改写成：

After the ship weighed anchor,she sailed out of the harbour.这就变为后照应了。又如：

Those who most deserve it rarely seem to suffer defeat. On his arrival in the capital the Foreign Minister declared his support for the government.在上述第一例中，人称代词it 出现在上文的关系分句(从属结构)中，而指代对象defeat则出现在下文，从而构成前照应关系。在第二例中，物主限定词his 出现在上文的介词词组中，指代对象 the Foreign Minister出现在下文，从而也构成前照应关系。这些前照应现象也都可以比较自由地为后照应所取代：

Those who most deserve defeat rarely seem to suffer it. On the Foreign Minister's arrival in the capital he declared support for the government.但是，后照应现象并不都能这样自由地为前照应所取代。试观察：

Mary bought a new dress, but she didn't like it. She bought a new dress, but Mary didn't like it.在上述第一句中，she和Mary是同一个人；而在第二句中，she的指代对象不是Mary,she和Mary是两个不同的人，从而它们之间没有照应关系。

4)指示照应指示照应是由指示代词和指示限定词(Demonstrative Determiner)实现的照应关系。

指示代词和指示限定词通常都可以比较自由地用于后照应。例如：

The platforms extend to the depth of 100 feet under the sea.These alone took 16 months to build. I'm going to buy the house.That will cost me a lot of money. Necessity is the mother of invention.This/That is remarkably true. Boxing matches were very popular in England 200 years ago. In those years, boxers fought with bare fists for prize money.指示代词 this/ these 也可用于前照应，但多半是指代下文中整个句子或若干句子。例如：

Listen to this. There should be no error,no delay,and no waste. This is what1 mean: You should have changed, not deleted, that paragraph. These are some of the things that must be done: New hospitals must be built. More parking space must be provided. The airports must be expanded. New schools must be built. Public transportation must be improved.

These language options(语言选修课)are open to our students: Spanish, French and German. that/ those很少用于前照应，偶尔使用，多带有讽刺意味(参见11.3.3)。例如：

l like that! He damaged my car and then blamed me for letting him use it. How do you like that? He stabs you in the back and then professes to be your friend. 5)代词照应在语篇中的应用既然代词的使用与它指代的对象有着不可分割的联系，因此在使用代词时，必须把它的指代对象明确地表示出来，句子的意义才不会有误。这不仅对于组词成句，而且对于组句成篇都有重要的意义。例如下面一则简短对话所用第三人称代词和物主代词等多达二十三次，但由于一开始便把指代对象明确地表示出来，整篇的意义也就明确无误：

Mr and Mrs Brown were talking about their neighbours, Mr and Mrs Smith, and their new house. "He must be making a good income to be able to live in a house like that," said he, "to say nothing of the car they have.It's a Rolls.""Oh,I don't think he makes much money," she replied, "but I fancy she has a private income.""I wonder whether they paid for it themselves or whether her parents gave it to her," he said. She answered, "Yes, they bought it after a lucky week with the football pools.But as for the car,I can't speak definitely about that,though I think it is hers rather than his.""I know which of the two l would sooner have," was his comment.又例如：

His Mother's Mate The haggard woman sat on a step under the electric light, by the entrance of the theatre. She had a child on one arm,two more beside her,a pile of papers on her knee, and a cigar box, full of matches, bootlaces and bone studs, on the pavement by her foot. A gentleman stepped out of the "marble bar" opposite, stood for a moment on the kerb, glanced at his watch and then across at the theatre. He crossed over, and put his hand in his pocket as he reached the pavement. "Paper, sir?" cried the newsboy. "Here,Y'are,mister—News, Star."But "mister" had noticed the woman and walked towards her.“Paper, sir! Star!” cried the boy, dodging in front; then with a quick glance from "mister's"face to the newswoman. "It's all right, mister! It's allthe same-she's my mother...Thanks."这是澳大利亚作家劳森(Henry Lawson)的一篇微型小说，言简意深，生动有力；其中人称照应关系严谨明晰，堪称典范。

下面做一则练习，要求正确处理代词的照应关系。

3.For instance,there are many children living in slum and poor areas with great potentials, but they are not enhanced because they feel they can't get ahead. 4. Since his father is an accountant,he is going to major in it. 5. When Bud Ellis met Jody Baker at the Recreation Centre, he challenged him to a game of chess. 6. Two weeks after Ed's uncle moved to Florida,he sold the laundromat. 9.Criminals pass in and out of jails as if they were equipped with revolving doors. 10. I put the frozen strawberries in the sink because it was leaking. 11.Mother asked Mrs Spry if she could visit her before she left for Europe. 12. Cabbage contains this vitamin, and it is essential to sound teeth. 13. Burkett was given the leading role in the play,which the other members of the cast did not like. 14. # 第12讲 动词和动词词组

自本讲至第23讲将进一步阐述动词和动词词组问题。本讲从动词分类说起并进一步交代“限定动词词组”和“非限定动词词组”。

英语动词按其在构成动词词组中所起的作用分为主动词(Main Verb)和助动词(Auxiliary)两大类。如前所述，动词词组是以主动词为中心词的词组，它可以仅由一个主动词构成，这叫做简单动词词组(Simple Verb Phrase)。例如：

He buys lemonade every time he goes shopping. The workers received a raise last year.动词词组也可由一个或一个以上的助动词加主动词构成，这叫做复杂动词词组(Complex Verb Phrase)。

例如：

She is visiting Copenhagen. They have been taking music lessons. You should have read the book. He needn'thave been waiting in the rain.由上述诸例可以看出，主动词构成动词词组的语义核心，它表示动词词组的基本意义，因此，主动词又叫“实义动词”(Notional Verb)。

助动词的语法功能是协助主动词表示不同的语法意义或情态意义，比如表示某一动作正在进行或已经完成，“应该”做某事或“不应该”做某事等意义。

## 12.1 助动词分类及其缩略形式

英语的助动词分为三类：基本助动词(Primary Auxiliary)、情态助动词(Modal Auxiliary)和半助动词(Semi-auxiliary)。

1)基本助动词基本助动词只有三个：be, do,have。作为助动词，be, do, have 本身没有词汇意义，只在动词词组中起语法作用或者说只表示语法意义。比如助动词be通常用来协助主动词构成进行体或被动态：

lam listening to a Beethoven symphony. 116 第12讲

The students were praised by the principal.助动词do通常用来协助主动词表示否定意义或构成疑问句：

My wife doesn'tknow Russian. What did she give to Halleck for his birthday?助动词do还可用来加重语气：

Ido think you'll have a hard job convincing him. You do look well. He did annoy everyone that evening. Do come and join us.助动词have通常用来协助主动词构成完成体或完成进行体：

Joan has seen that movie. I have been working here for 20 years. 2)情态助动词情态助动词共有14个，其中包括一些过去时形式。它们是can/ could, may/might, will /would, shall / should, must, ought to, dare / dared, need, used to。情态助动词表示情态意义，其过去时形式并不一定就表示过去时间。情态助动词不能重叠使用；随后的主动词无一例外地是不带 to的不定式即动词原形。例如：

It may snow before nightfall. Brenda grew up in Naples. She must know how to speak Italian. Stainless steel will resist corrosion. Would you let me use your pen a minute? When lived at Del Mar,l used to take a long walk along the footpath under the pine trees. If she became a movie star, she could afford a new home.关于“情态助动词”的用法，将在第17,18,19三讲系统交代。

3)半助动词所谓“半助动词”(Semi-auxiliary)就是在语法作用上介乎主动词和助动词之间的、并能表示情态意义的语法结构，所以又叫“半情态助动词”(Semi-modal)。

从形态上看，半助动词可分为三类：一类是以be为其中心成分；一类是以have为其中心成分；一类是以seem等为其中心成分。从能否与 it...that-clause结构进行转换看，又可分为两小类：第一小类不可与it...that句型转换，而第二小类可作这样的转换。列表如下：

第一小类 第二小类be about to be certain to be able to be(un)likely to be apt to appear to be bound to chance to be due to happen to be going to seem to be liable to tum out to, etc be obliged to be supposed to be sure to be to

(续表) be wiling to had better/best have to have got to come to fail to get to tend to, etc例如：

Cast iron(生铁)is apt to break. You are bound to succeed. We are all liable to make mistakes occasionally. James will be obliged to resign. I was to have seen him last Wednesday but he didn't come. How did you come to know where she is living? He never fails to write to his mother every week. How did you get to know that I was here? You'd better be working harder when the boss comes back. I thought I'd best have your opinion first. He is certain to come. Are we likely to arrive on time? He is unlikely to win the race. He appears to have many friends. Ichanced to be out when she called. She happened to be met in the street by the boy. Things far off seem to be small. It tumed out to be a fine day.以上最后数例的半助动词属于第二小类，它们都能与It... that-clause互相转换：

It is likely/unlikely that they will succeed.→ They are likely/unlikely to succeed. It appears that he has many friends here.→He appears to have many friends here. It seems that he is enjoying himself.→He seems to be enjoying himself. It happens that I have had some nasty experiences in dealing with him.→I happen to have had some nasty experiences in dealing with him. 4)助动词的缩略形式英语有以下24个助动词能有缩略形式(Contracted Form):④am, is, are,was, were, have, has, had, do, does, did, shall, should, will, would, can, could, may,①这里所说的缩略形式主要指基本助动词和情态助动词的缩略形式。鉴于动词 be和have的某些形式作主动词时也能有缩略形式，且较常见，有其实用性，将附带一起处理。

118 第12讲

might, must, ought(to), need, dare, used (to) a)否定缩略形式上述24个限定动词除去am not的疑问形式需用aren't(1)替代而外，其余23个都有各自的否定缩略形式： hadn't/h?dnt/ can't/ka:nt/am not/(a:nt)/ dont/d?unt/ couldn't/kudnt/aren't/a:nt/ doesn't/d?znt/ mayn't/ment/isn't/rznt/ didn't/ddnt/ mightn't/martnt/wasn't/wDznt/ shan't/?a:nt/ mustn't/m?snt/weren't/ws:nt/ won't/waunt/ oughtn't/?:tnt/haven't/h?vnt/ shouldn't /fudnt/ needn't/ni:dnt/hasn't/ha?znt/ wouldn't/wudnt/ daren't/deont/usedn't/ju:snt/兹各举一例如下： We shan't go. I'm late,aren't1? They won't be here next week. We aren't ready yet. You shouldn't smoke so much. He isn't going anywhere tonight. John wouldn't do a thing like that! He wasn't home last night. He cant do it. They weren'tout of town last week. Icouldn't swim a year ago. They haven't had time to get there. They mayn't like it. Hehasn't written again, has he? Itmightn't be true. Ihadn't got a penny in my pocket. Youmustn't pick the flowers in the park. Idon't often do things like that. Ineedn'thave gone to the station. She doesn't like it. John daren't go back alone. He didn't see hertillthis week. You usedn't to worry about things like that.有些否定句式能有两种缩略形式可供选择：

She isn't coming here alone. She's not coming here alone.否定疑问句既能用否定缩略形式，也能用非缩略形式。试比较：

Haven'twe seen this film before? Have we not seen this film before?但含义有所不同：用否定缩略形式通常带有肯定与否定双重意向；用非缩略否定形式通常带有否定意向。

(参见28.2.1) b)肯定缩略形式上述二十四个限定动词只有以下十个能有肯定缩略形式：

am('m)/m,am/ have('ve)/v,av/ shall('I)/1,(a)I/is('s)/z,s/ has('s)/z,s/ will('I)/1,(a)/are('re)/a,or/ had('d)/d,ad/ should('d)/d,ad/would('d)/d, ad/下面介绍一下肯定缩略形式与其他词语的常见搭配现象。

第一，与人称代词搭配。例如：

I'ma student. She's been away for months. We're playing bridge this evening. I' tell him. It's raining. They'll have to come home. Ive got a lot of things to tell you. He'd gone when I arrived.

He said you'd had a car accident. P'd be grateful if you'd lend me your car. They'd stay in this hotel if it was cheaper. its和its同音异义，不能混淆。试比较：

The cat is licking its tail;its not licking its paw. he's, she's, its=he is/he has, she is/she has, it is/it has,需从上下文予以判别。试比较：

He's here.(is) It's been snowing since midnight.(has) 'd既可表示should, would,也可表示 had,读音相同，需从上下文予以判别。试比较：

Pd finished before he came.(had) We'd better hurry up. (had) They'd prefer to go now, but we'd rather go later this evening. (would, would) We'd have come if you'd asked us.(should/would, had)第二，与名词词组搭配。例如：

Mary's coming tomorrow evening. Anna'll be going to London. That child's sitting in the middle of the road. The manager'll see you soon. Canada's got enormous forests. The camera'd been stolen. My friend's opened anew hotel. The book'd be useful. The roads're good. The guests've all gone. The teachers of English're coming. The boys with the top marks've got prizes.第三，与指示代词、不定代词、物主代词搭配。例如：

That's fine. Something's got to be done. This'/ do. Nobody'll do that for you. That'd be much better. Mine's blue; yours is red. One's enough.第四，与存在句引导词 there搭配。例如：

There's a lot to do. There's been a farm here for centuries. There've been too many accidents at this comer. There'l/ be a new film next week. She wondered if there'd been a mistake. He thought there'd be an answer by tomorrow.地点副词here/there也常与's(=is)搭配。例如：

Here's your cup. There's your car. Here's your bicycle and there's mine.第五，与疑问词搭配。例如：

How's your father? Who's here? What's happening? Why's he coming? What're you doing? What'W you have to drink? When's the race going to start? Who'd have believed it? Where've they gone to? 120 第12讲

### EXERCISE 12A

Rephrase the following, using proper semi-auxiliaries: 1. It is likely that he will let you down(拆你台，使你失望), 2. It is certain that our team will win. 3. It appears that there is no doubt about it. 4. It happened that John was the only witness. 5. It chanced that I was out of London at the time. 6. It seems that John's not coming after all. 7. We're not allowed to play football on Sundays. 8.Tim hasn't arrived yet.What's wrong? He should have arrived at five. 9.He'll certainly be there. 10. They'll certainly need help. 11. I think it'll rain this evening. 12.How can I pay my debts? 13.The new building will be six storeys high. 14.You mustn't talk like that. 15. He probably will see her. 16. You must be joking. 17.He can be stupid at times. 18.I do not see what you mean. 19. We were there by accident. 20.I met him by chance in the park. 21.He apparently enjoyed the concert. 22.Seemingly they did not notice it. 23. The swimmer did not reach the shore. 24. We had agreed to marry in May(but didn't marry). 25. The purse could not be found. 26. It was his destiny never to see his wife or children again. 27.It will be a long time before we can reduce our prices. 28. John must be in the office. 29. None of us can say what our future will be. 30.He did keep his word.

## 12.2 主动词分类

英语的主动词可从以下不同角度进行分类。

1)及物动词、不及物动词、连系动词主动词按其是否必须跟有补足成分(Complementation)以及必须跟有什么样的补足成分如宾语、补语、状语等，分为及物动词(Transitive Verb)、不及物动词(Intransitive Verb)和连系动词(Linking Verb)。

a)及物动词及物动词之后须带宾语(Object)。只带一个宾语的及物动词叫做“单宾及物动词”(Monotransitive Verb)。例如：

John is playing cricket. Mark researched his topic thoroughly.有些及物动词可带双宾语，即间接宾语(Indirect Object)和直接宾语(Direct Object)。能带双宾语的及物动词叫做“双宾及物动词”(Ditransitive Verb)。例如：

The new lamp gave us more light. Celeste paid me a compliment.有些及物动词在一定上下文中须带宾语和宾语补语(Object Complement)。此类及物动词叫做“复宾及物动词”(Complex-transitive Verb)。例如：

The jury found the defender not guilty. You should keep the room clean all the time.有些及物动词在一定上下文中不仅须带宾语，而且宾语之后还须带状语。例如：

The Chinese treat older people with great respect. put the book on the shelf. b)不及物动词不及物动词之后不须带宾语。例如：

The president is speaking. The hunter suddenly stopped. He heard a noise in the bushes.但是，有些不及物动词在一定上下文中，其后须带状语，否则意义不完全。例如：

Betty and Sue have lived in Atlanta since 1975. Daffodils bloom in early spring. Frank cheats at poker. The musician perfoms in Boston next month. c)连系动词连系动词之后须带主语补语(Subject Complement)。例如：

The capital of the United States was once New York City. When did you become suspicious? The puddle got bigger the more it rained. That sounds like a good idea. She is now feeling much better. The photograph measures 8by 10 inches. The fish smells awful.以上三类动词的划分并不是绝对的，许多动词都有跨类现象：在一种上下文中是及物动词，在另一种上下文中却是不及物动词或连系动词。例如：

The fish is fresh from water.You don't have to smell it. The milk is going bad. It smells. This dish smells delicious. smell 在以上第一例中是及物动词，在第二例中是不及物动词，而在第三例中则是连系动词。

2)动态动词与静态动词英语动词，按其词汇意义，又可分为动态动词(Dynamic Verb)和静态动词(Stative Verb)。动态动词是表示运动状态的动词，而静态动词则是表示一种相对静止状态的动词。

a)动态动词动态动词，按词汇意义，又可分为3个小类：一是表示持续动作的动词，如drink, eat,fly,play, rain, read, run, sit, stand, sleep, talk,watch,write, work 等。这类动词既可用于非进行体，也可用于进行体。例如：

122 第12讲

She works at a chemical factory. She has been working there for a long time. They talked and talked until midnight. They were talking about the house.二是表示改变或移动的动词，如arrive, become,change, come,get,go,grow,leave,reach, turn等。这类动词也是既可用于非进行体，也可用于进行体。例如：

Winter is here. The leaves of the trees are tuming yellow. I tumed my head and saw the profile of a man. The weather is changing for the better. Shanghai has changed a lot in the past 10 years.三是表示短暂动作的动词，如hit,jump, kick,knock,open/ close (a door),put(something on the table),shut, take out等。这类动词可用一般现在时表示说话时正在进行的短暂动作，若用进行体则表示短暂动作的不断重复。例如：

(舞台说明)The old man stops at a house and knocks at the door. Who is knocking at the door?这类动词若用一般过去时，则表示发生在过去的一次性短暂动作，若用过去进行体，也表示短暂动作的不断重复。例如：

He opened the door and ran out of the house. He was opening and closing the door to make sure that it worked properly. b)静态动词静态动词表示存在于现时或过去的一种状态。这种动词的最主要语法特征就是通常用于非进行体，若用进行体，通常会引起语义的改变。这类动词，按词汇意义，又可分为4小类：第一类是用作主动词的be和have(作“有”解)。例如：

Jim is a teacher, but 20 years ago he was a soldier. We have friends all over the world.第二类是含有静态动词 be和have意义的动词，如 apply to(适用于),belong to, differ from, cost, weigh, measure,fit(适合),hold(可容纳),lack,resemble等。例如：

This rule applies to(=is applicable to) everyone. This house belongs to my brother(= is my brothers property). French differs from(=is different from)English in having gender for all nouns. The auditorium holds (=has a capacity for)2000 people.第三类是表示感觉的动词，如 feel, hear, see,smell, taste等。例如：

She doesn'thear very well. I(can)see the snow-capped peaks of the distant mountains. The material feels soft. The roses smell fragrant. I(can)taste pepper in the soup. It tastes hot.第四类是表示心理或情感状态的动词，如assume, believe, consider(= think),detest,fear, hate, hope, imagine,know,like, love, mean, mind, notice, prefer, regret, remember, suppose, think, understand, want, wish等。例如：

I believe we have met before. Jim knows Chinese. We understand your difficulty. How I wish I could visit the Grand Canyon!

如前所述，静态动词的最主要语法特征是不用于进行体，若用进行体往往会改变含义，即变成了动态动词。例如：

He is being foolish (=is acting foolishly). We're having a wonderful time(=are enjoying ourselves).又例如某些属于第二类的静态动词，一旦用于进行体也就变成了动态动词。比较：

He resembles his father. He is resembling his father(=is becoming more and more like his father)as the years go by. This camera costs 140 dollars. This mistake is costing us dearly(=is bringing great injury to us).又例如，当表示感觉的动词用于表示主动含义时，便是动态动词。比较：

I(can)feel a pin in it somewhere./I'm feling(摸)this cushion. I(can)taste pepper init./I'm tasting(品尝)this soup. see和hear用于进行体也通常改变意义。例如：

lam hearing(=attending) lectures at the university. He is seeing(=visiting)the sights.但是，表示肉体感觉的动词，如ache,feel, hurt等，用进行体和不用进行体均可，含义不变。例如：

My foot aches/is aching. I don't feel/am not feeling very tired.表示心理或情感状态的动词用于进行体时通常改变含义：

Be quiet. I'm thinking(=giving thought to a problem). Surely you're imagining things(=entertaining or indulging yourself with illusions). Now you're understanding(=beginning to understand)this a little better.但在口语中，间或也有用进行体而不改变含义的情况。例如：

How do you like this hot weather?/How are you liking this hot weather?还有少数几个动词用进行体表示婉转口气的用法也属于这一类情况。例如：

Were you wanting to see me? I'm hoping you'll look after the children for us. 3)单词动词与词组动词英语主动词，按其构成，可分为单词动词(Single-word Verb)和词组动词(Phrasal Verb)。单词动词是由一个单词构成的动词，如walk, take, beautify,modernize,enable,embody 等。前一节所举诸例，基本上都是单词动词。本节着重介绍词组动词。

词组动词指由两个或两个以上单词构成的动词。这种动词主要分为以下三类：

a)动词+介词由动词+介词构成的动词词项在本书第1-2版曾叫做“介词动词”,自3版起，为了简化术语，统称为“词组动词”。由动词+介词构成的词组动词数量很大，兹略举数例如下：

He couldn't account for(解释)his long absence from school. lagree with(与⋯⋯看法一致) you on that point. She doesn't agree to(同意)my proposal. His comments amount to(等于)treason. When his father died, Jim came into(继承) a fortune. In the valley we came across(碰到) a group of Mexicans. It is the job of the police to get after(追捕)criminals. If you go about(做)it in the right way,you'll soon get it finished. IfI were you, I would go for(争取)that job. 124 第12讲

The police are looking into(调查)the case. They didn't look after(照顾)the children properly. Can we relyon(信赖)this old car to get us there? When the child's pleas didn't work, he resorted to(诉诸)tears. If you are too busy, I'll see about(料理)train tickets. Who is seeing to(料理)the arrangements for the next meeting? Why do you take me for(把⋯⋯当做)a fool? Before long he had taken to(爱上)a girl student in his class. b)动词+副词小品词在这一类词组动词中的副词并不起修饰作用，所以又叫“副词小品词”。例如：

The plane blew up(爆炸) when it was hit by a missile. The Sino-Japanese War broke out(爆发)in 1937. The boy was well brought up(教养). We are going to bring up(提出)some important problems to discuss at the meeting. The traffic to the beach started to build up(增长)at noon. The meeting has been called off(取消). After just a few minutes she suddenly came to(苏醒过来). We will carry on(继续)our conversation tomorrow. The plan has been satisfactorily camied out(贯彻). The question of what time to be there never came up(发生). Some new problems cropped up(冒出)at the last minute. This paper comes out(出版)once a week. Can you figure out(想出)a way to do it? I can't figure out(理解)why he said that. He fared up(发脾气) at me when advised him to give up smoking. The firm had to lay off(解雇)100 men. They are quarrelling and making up(和好)by turns. We must do all we can to make up(弥补)the economic losses. We could hardly make out(理解)what she meant. My investment in these stocks had really paid off(赢利). Uncle Herman passed away(去世)many years ago. She is not really that way. She is justputting on(装模作样). Take the watch apart(拆开)and see if you can see what's wrong with it. Even the most experienced eye may be taken in(受骗上当)on certain occasions. We have decided to take on(雇用)a new clerk in the accounts department. Our firm took over(接管)2 fruit packing companies last year. c)动词+副词小品词+介词这类动词词项在本书第1-2版曾叫做“词组介词动词”,自3版起也是仅作为词组动词的一种。例如：

Willthis enthusiasm cary over to(持续到)the next week? I finally caught on to(理解)what she was talking about. I don't want to come down with(染上)the flu again. My nephew often comes out with(说出)the cleverest remarks. I will have to fill in for(顶替)Wally until he gets back. Put the food where the cat can'tget around to/at(够得到).

How could heget away with(得逞)cheating? My wife is always getting on at(责怪)me for not keeping the room clean. I'vegot on to(想出)a good idea for increasing production. The children didn't quite get on to(理解)what the teacher was saying. We don'tgo in for(喜欢)that kind of thing. I can'tgo through with(完成)the performance. I'm so nervous. His English is not good enough for him to keep up with(跟上)the rest of the class. I couldn'tput up with(忍受)the noise any longer. The children are looking forward to(盼望)the holiday. We all lok up to(尊敬)Roger. He's authoritative but kind. We should not look down on(看不起)their humble food. You must make up for(弥补)the omission. I willow up to(承认)my mistakes. You could be more convincing if you didn't talk down to(对⋯⋯大言不惭地说话)your audience.以上三类词组动词都是一些固定搭配，相当于一个及物的或不及物的单词动词，因而又称为“成语动词”(Verbal Idiom)。

还要注意其他一些属于成语性质的固定搭配。这类搭配在有些语法著作中也归入“词组动词”之列。

这类词组动词有的是“动词+名词+介词”,相当于一个及物动词，如make fun of(取笑),make the most of(充分利用),make a mess of(把⋯⋯弄脏、弄糟),take care of(照顾，照料),take advantage of(利用)等；有的是“动词+名词”,相当于一个不及物动词，如change hands(易手，过户),take effect(生效),take root(扎根),take place(发生)等。例如：

She soon realized that she was being made fun of. You should make the most of the opportunity to improve your English. She indulged in luxury and made amess of her life. I will take care of your children when you are away. They are taking full advantage of the favourable situation to increase exports. The house has changed hands several times. In a few minutes the medicine will take effect. Very soon the seeds germinated and took root. His ideas have taken root in the minds of his followers. When did the accident take place? 4)规则动词与不规则动词大多数动词的过去时和-ed 分词都是在原形之后加词尾-ed 构成(如 intend / intended/intended, study/studied/studied, stop/stopped/stopped),这类动词叫做规则动词(Regular Verb)。有一些动词不以加词尾-ed的方式构成过去时和-ed分词(如cut/ cut/cut,build / built/ built, begin/began/begun),这类动词叫不规则动词(Irregular Verb)。

现代英语的绝大多数动词是规则动词，新近增添的动词一般也都是规则动词。现有的不规则动词总数不过二百多个。这些不规则动词分为三种类型：

第一类不规则动词的特点是它们的三个主要形式(即原形、过去时、-ed分词)同形。例如：

cast cast cast hit hit hit put put put set set set spread spread spread 126 第12讲

第二类不规则动词的特点是它们的过去时与-ed分词同形。例如：

bend bent bent creep crept crept deal dealt dealt flee fled fled mean meant meant第三类不规则动词的特点是它们的三个主要形式都不相同。例如：

arise arose arisen break broke broken ring rang rung steal stole stolen throw threw thrown

Sophie明天将请一天假，我必须顶替她。(fill in for) 15.我们打算在下一次会议上提出这个问题。(bring up) 16.我盼望在上海见到你。(look forward to) 17.我们都尊敬林医生，因为她忘我地为病人服务。(look up to) 18.他在美国工作了三年，并充分利用这个机会来提高他的英语水平。(make the most of) 19.她太懒了，把住房弄得一塌糊涂。(make a mess of) 20.这是一辆旧车，已经转手好几次了。(change hands)

## 12.3 限定动词(词组)与非限定动词(词组)

在这里，首先要解释一下什么叫“限定”(finite)与“非限定”(non-finite)。这是两个关于动词形式的术语：所谓 finite 意为of a verb form that agrees with its subject in Person and Number and indicates the time of the action or state expressed by the verb(指一种动词形式，它在人称和数的方面与主语保持一致并标示出该动词所表示的动作或状态发生在什么时间。)而“非限定”则表示不具有以上特质。

1)限定动词与非限定动词限定动词(Finite Verb)和非限定动词(Non-finite Verb)又叫动词的限定形式(Finite Form)和非限定形式(Non-finite Form)。英语的主动词出现在词典或词汇表中时通常是以“原形”(Base Form)出现的，但当它出现在句中时，便有5种语法形式(Grammatical Form):2种限定形式，即现在时(Present Tense)和过去时(Past Tense),和3种非限定形式，即不定式(Infinitive)、-ing分词和-ed分词。因此，限定动词与非限定动词之间最重要的区别在于前者有“时”(Tense)的标记，而后者没有“时”的标记。举例列表如下：

128 第12讲

原形 现在时 过去时 不定式 -ing分词 -ed分词walk walk(s) walked (to) walk walking walked pass pass(es) passed (to)pass passing passed eat eat(s) ate (to) eat eating eaten speak speak(s) spoke (to) speak speaking spoken teach teach(es) taught (to) teach teaching taught grow grow(s) grew (to) grow growing grown英语的基本助动词和情态助动词除个别外并非都具有以上6种形式。列表如下：

原形 现在时 过去时 不定式 -ing分词 -ed分词be is/am/are was/were (to) be being been have has/have had (to) have having do does/do did can could may might willshall wouldshould 一must need dare dared一一 ought toused to 一 一 一2)限定动词词组与非限定动词词组如前所述，动词词组是以主动词为中心词的词组。动词词组，按其是否带有“时”的标记分为限定动词词组(Finite Verb Phrase)和非限定动词词组(Non-finite Verb Phrase)。

a)限定动词词组如前所述(参见1.3.2),动词词组可以仅由一个主动词构成，也可由一个或一个以上助动词+主动词构成。不管怎么样，只要构成动词词组的第一个词是个限定动词，这便是限定动词词组。现在让我们看一看限定动词词组的结构形式：假定主语为第三人称单数，主动词为do,那么,由八种时体形式的主动态和被动态构成的动词词组是：

时体形式 主动态 被动态一般现在时 does is done一般过去时 did was done现在进行体 is doing is being done过去进行体 was doing was being done现在完成体 has done has been done过去完成体现在完成进行体过去完成进行体 had donehas been doinghad been doing —had been done—一

由上表可知，限定动词词组由主动词单独构成时，其“时”的特征便体现在主动词上；由“助动词+主动词”构成时，其“时”的特征体现在助动词上；有一个以上助动词时则体现在第一个助动词上。

b)非限定动词词组凡由不定式、-ing 分词或-ed分词等非限定动词开首的动词词组叫做非限定动词词组。除-ed分词外，不定式和-ing 分词能有“体”的形式，其中非进行体也能有被动态。现在也以主动词do为例，由不定式开首的动词词组有以下几种形式：

不定式 主动态 被动态一般形式进行体 (to) do(to)be doing (to) be done—一完成体完成进行体 (to) have done(to) have been doing —一(to)have been done由-ing分词开首的动词词组有以下几种形式：

-ing分词 主动态 被动态一般形式 doing being done完成体 having done having been done关于限定动词词组和非限定动词词组的用法将在随后几讲中详细阐述。

试比较：

The man is not to be bluffed.(限定动词词组) He is not a man to be bluffed.(非限定动词词组) Old Tom delivers letters and newspapers all the year round.(限定动词词组) His duty is to deliver letters and newspapers.(非限定动词词组) As his father was ill, John had to look after the shop.(限定动词词组) His father being ill, John had to look after the shop.(非限定动词词组) This mixture consists of oil and vinegar.(限定动词词组) It's a mixture consisting of oil and vinegar.(非限定动词词组) Although we were exhausted by the climb, we determined to continue the journey.(限定动词词组) Though exhausted by the climb, we determined to continue the journey.(非限定动词词组) As the old man was bom and bred in the countryside, he was not accustomed to city life.(限定动词词组) Bom and bred in the countryside, the old man was not accustomed to city life.(非限定动词词组)下面做一则练习，要求填入所给动词的适当形式。

Side by side they (5)____(stand). A big sign above one(6)___(read)"whites only". The sign above the other fountain 130 第12讲

(7)______(read) "colored only". I(8)____(decide) that once and for all I would(9)____(satisfy) my curiosity about the water fountains which always(10)______(carry) the signs above them. Was the water (11)____(color)in the “colored only” one, or did it(12)____(taste) different?
