Let's go,_____?

## 28.3祈使句和感叹句

本节介绍祈使句(Command)和感叹句(Exclamation)。

1)祈使句祈使句(Command,又叫 Imperative Sentence)通常是以动词祈使式(Imperative Mood)开首，表示命令、指示、要求、建议、劝告等意义的特殊句式。现代英语表示命令、指示等意义有多种多样的表达方

式，比如可以用陈述句来表达：

I wish you'd open the window.(请求) The colonel wants these barracks cleaned now.(指示) The senator needs you in his office immediately.(命令) I hope you are going to take your muddy shoes off before you get inside.(劝告)也可用疑问句的形式来表达：

Would you close the door, please?(请求) Could you tum the music down a little?(劝告) Could I see you for a moment?(boss to subordinate)(命令)当然也可用一种名叫“祈使句”的特殊句式来表达：

Open the window,please. Clean these barracks now. Go to the senator's office immediately. Take off your muddy shoes before you get inside.现代英语祈使句有三种类型，分述如下。

a)第二人称祈使句第二人称祈使句是以听话人为祈使对象的祈使句。在这种祈使句中，祈使对象you通常不表示出来，句子以动词祈使式开首，用降调，句尾用句号或感叹号。例如：

Come and have dinner with us. Look out! There is a bus!这种祈使句的否定形式是在句首加 Don't或Do not(用于正式语体)或其他否定词。例如：

Don't be nervous! Do not feed the animals!(动物园的告示) Never do that again!为使祈使句的口气缓和一些，可在句首或句尾加please,或在句尾增添附加问句。例如：

Please sit down. Stop talking,please. Lend me your penknife please, wilyou? Help me with this maths problem,won'tyou/would you/cantyou/could you? Don't be so noisy, will you? Go to the party with us, why don'tyou?为使祈使句的口气强硬一些，可在句首加上重读的助动词 do。例如：

'Do have a glass of sherry. 'Do stop talking! 'Do be careful of my broken leg.有时为了强调对谁提出要求或发出指示，可将第二人称祈使对象you表示出来，这种you要重读。例如：

'You take that seat,and I'l take this one. 'You mind your own business and leave this to me! Don't'you forget i! Don't'you behave like that!有时还可在you 之后再加上一个表示“义务”的情态助动词will,进一步提高祈使口气的强硬度。例如：

'You will mind your own business! This is school time.'You will wait outside the gate.有时为了强调命令或指示对谁而发，还可在祈使句中加上呼语(Vocative)。例如：

328 第28讲

Stop talking, boys. Jack, stand over there. Be quiet, Tom. Everyone keep quiet. Don't anybody behave like that!这些带呼语的祈使句，其隐含的祈使对象还是you。

b)第一人称祈使句如果说话人以自己为祈使对象，便可用第一人称祈使句。第一人称祈使句是以Let为引导词，随后跟单数第一人称代词宾格(me)+不定式。例如：

Let me try again. Let me drive you home. Let me think what to do next.这种祈使句之后还可带附加问句。例如：

Let me drive you home, shall ? Let me have a look, can/?如果祈使对象包括说话人和听话人在内，便可用“Lets+不定式”结构。例如：

Lets stop now and finish it later. Lets spend a day in the country.如果要带附加问句，便须用shall we。例如：

Lets take a taxi, shall we? Lets go fishing this aftemoon, shall we?第一人称祈使句的否定形式是在句首加Don't。例如：

Don't let me bother you too much. Don't let me interrupt you. Don't let's argue about it. Don't let's invite George to the party;he is such a bore!如果用于正式场合，便在Let me/Lets之后加 not。例如：

Let me not bother you too much. Lets not say anything about it ill we have the facts.第一人称祈使句的强调形式也是在句首加上重读的助动词do。例如：

'Do let me have a look! Do let's stop arguing! c)第三人称祈使句第三人称祈使句也是以Let为引导词，随后跟第三人称代词宾格(或名词词组)+不定式，它的结构模式是：Let him/them/NP+不定式。例如：

Let bygones be bygones.(既往不咎。) Let the boys clear up this mess. Let the guards be armed. Don't let him disturb us. Don't let anyone shirk his responsibility.(谁也不要逃避责任。)要注意，“Let him/ them+不定式”结构在特定的语境中也可能并不是第三人称祈使句，而是第二人称祈使句。例如：

Let him come with us, mother; I'l ake care of him.=Please allow him to come with us;.⋯. Let her go with you, will you? 2)感叹句感叹句(Exclamation,又叫Exclamatory Sentence)是一种特殊的表达强烈感情的句式。表示强烈感情，有多种多样的手段。分述如下：

a) What-型和How-型的感叹句这是比较常见的感叹句形式，通常用低降调；有时为了表达更强烈的感情，也可用高降调。例如：

'What a relief! 'How ri、diculous! What an extraordinary 'thing to `do! The 'best of `luck to you! b)问句型的感叹句问句型的感叹句(Interrogative Exclamation)通常用低降调，常见于否定结构。例如：

'Aren't they lovely! 'Wasn'tit'absolutely.marvellous! 'Would you be.lieve it! Isn't he ,kind!问句型的感叹句有时只由一个感叹词构成，主要用高升调。例如：

: ohn's bak A:'Sheila's'passed here`xam. B: "Really? A:'Fanny's 'going onasa'fari. lB:'What? c)重复感叹句重复感叹句(Echo Exclamation)通常用高降调，表示惊讶、怀疑等强烈感情。例如：

A:I'm'going to 'London fora、holiday. B: To 'London! 'That's not `my idea of a rest. A:What a'beautiful day! B: 'Beautiful`day! You `must be joking. d)such-型/so-型感叹句此类感叹句一般也都用降调，表示惊讶、赞美、厌恶等感情。例如：

We've had `such a good,time! It was `such a long time a.go! If'only I'hadn't been so 'careless! The'garden looks so lovely to,day! 330 第28讲

# 第29讲 存在句

存在句(Existential Sentence)是一种表示“存在”的特殊句型。它的主要交际功能是传递新信息，引出新话题。这种句型之所以要用 there作形式主语而把“实义主语”置于动词之后正是为了让这种“实义主语”成为信息中心，以达到引出新话题之目的。

## 29.1 存在句的结构特征-

存在句是以非重读there/8a/作形式主语，谓语动词通常是主动词 be或其他含有“存在”意义的动词的一定形式；动词之后跟有通常叫做“实义主语”(Notional Subject)或“真主语”(Real Subject)的名词词组；“实义主语”之后通常跟有地点状语(Locative Adverbial),有时还带时间状语(Temporal Adverbial)。因此，存在句的结构模式是：

There+be+NP+Locative Expression(+Temporal Expression) 1)存在句的形式主语存在句的引导词 there在句子中居于主语的位置，起形式主语的作用，在疑问句中，它和操作词倒装：

Are there any people in the hall? There is a computer in the room, isn't there?在非正式语体中，引导词 there还可决定主谓一致关系，即动词的单、复数形式间或并不取决于随后的名词词组而取决于引导词 there。例如：

There's some people in the waiting room.(非正式语体) There are some people in the waiting room.(正式语体)要区别存在句的形式主语 there和句首状语 there,前者不重读，后者须重读。比较：

There /or/is no bus now.(形式主语 there) 'There /ee?r/is our bus.(状语 there) There/8o/ are too many people here.(形式主语there) 'There/8e?/ are our friends.(状语 there)

2)存在句的谓语动词存在句的谓语动词主要是动词be的某种形式，可以是限定形式，也可以是非限定形式。先讲be的限定形式。限定形式可以是be的一般现在时、一般过去时、现在完成体或过去完成体。例如：

There's a train due to arrive. There have been many such accidents.也可以是表示“预见”、“可能”、“必然”或“过去习惯存在”的情态助动词+不定式(包括完成体形式)。例如：

There'll be a huge crowd at the game, won't there? There can be very little doubt about his guilt. Mustn't there be another reason for his behavior? There used to be a church round the corner.{A:Was there an answer to my telegram? B:John says there must have been one. There can'thave been much traffic so late at night.也可以是半助动词+不定式(很少用完成体形式)。例如：

There's going to be a storm. There's to be an investigation. There appears to be no doubt about it.以上三类动词形式是限定形式的主体。除be外，某些表示存在概念的不及物动词如 exist, remain, happen,occur, come, arise, stand, lie, live等，也能用于 there-存在句。例如：

There arose in his imagination visions of a world empire. If ever again there happens an accident like that, we'll have only ourselves to blame.如果地点状语前移便可省略 there。例如：

Behind the house (there) lies a river. In front of the carriage(there) rode two men in uniform.上述诸例的谓语动词都可用be的某种形式替代。

值得注意的是：以上诸例中的主动词都带有“存在”的意思；反之，如果动词不表示“存在”、“出现”之类的意义，便不能用作存在句的谓语动词。试比较：

There appeared/emerged ship after ship from afar.× There disappeared/went down ship after ship in the distance. There blew a gale. x There subsided a gale. There was approaching a weird procession(奇装游行队伍).* There departed a weird procession. There is just being decoded(被破译)an important message.× There is just being encoded(被译成代码) an important message. There sailed ina ship.× There sailed awaya ship. 3)存在句的实义主语在存在句中作实义主语的名词词组通常是非确定特指，即通常带有不定冠词、零冠词及其他表示非确定意义的限定词，如some,any,no,several, many, much, more,(a)few,(a)little, less, another,a lot of,plenty of,a(large) number of,enough,以及基数词等，而且名词中心词可以带有前置、后置修饰语。例如：

There isa cherry tree in my garden. 332 第29讲

There are roses in the flower beds. There's no one in the house,is there? There is still a lot of work to be done before the house is ready for occupation. There are many people sil with too low a standard of living. There are fifteen students in my class. Is there any cheese in the larder?作为存在句实义主语的名词词组也可由some-,any-,no-的合成词构成。例如：

There's something (that) you don't know. There is nothing more to do. Is there anyone coming to dinner?由上述诸例可以看出，存在句的实义主语一般都是非确定特指，因为存在句的实义主语本来就是句子的信息中心，即所要传递的新信息，从而不可能如当代某个语法流派所云是由原先出现在句首主语位置上的表示非确定特指的名词词组移位而来。试观察：

There isahair in my soup.?A hair is in my soup. There'san accident outside.?An accident has been outside. There seems to bea small problem here.?A small problem seems to be here. There's a lady outside asking for you.?A lady is outside asking for you. I don't want there to be any more animosity.?I don't want any more animosity to be.当然，如果带 a/an 作限定词的名词词组不是用于非确定特指，而是用于类指，当然可以出现在句首。

例如：

A tree is a living organism. An ox is a domestic animal.既然存在句的实义主语通常都是表示非确定特指的名词词组，因此在脱离一定上下文的情况下，如果实义主语带有确定特指的含义，往往就会造成病句。例如：

× There is the/his/ this car at the door.* There's the money borrowed from the bank.但是，在特定的语境中，在不违背“传递新信息”的前提下，存在句带有确定特指实义主语的可能性还是存在的。例如：

A:What can we carry this shopping in? B: There is the/my/this briefcase. Wil that be all right?上文问的是“我们买的这些东西，拿什么装啊?”下文回答说“就装在我的公文包里，行吗?”在这个存在句中，实义主语虽是确定特指，但它是为了回答以上what-问句，仍然是提供新信息，从而是可接受的。又例如：

A:Who of all our friends can we trust? B: There's only John, I'm afraid.以上人名本身已带有确定特指的含义，本来是不可以用作存在句的实义主语，但是在以上对话中，人名是用来回答问题，获得了新信息的身份，所以在存在句中可以照用不误。人称代词的使用也有类似的情形。

例如：

A:Let's not forget Bill. B:Oh,yes, there'shim—and there's you and me—that ought to make up the number we need.

4)存在句的句尾结构存在句的句尾通常带有地点状语和/或时间状语，借以表示在何处、何时存在着或发生了某种情况或事态。例如：

If you are looking for a café,there is one opposite the station. There are 51 states in the USA. There used to be a church round the comer. There will be an interval of 20 minutes during the performance. There was a strong wind here last night.存在句的句尾还可带有非限定分句和关系分句。例如名词词组+不定式分句：

There is work to employ thousands of people.=There is work that could employ thousands of people. There are always new things to be discovered.=There are always new things that are to be discovered. There is a man to show you round the castle.= There is a man whose duty is to show you round the castle. There's plenty of work to do.= There's plenty of work that people must do.名词词组+-ing分词分句；There is a man sitting on the fence. There's a taxi coming round the comer.名词词组+-ed分词分句：

There was a ship sunk last night. There are two scales of temperature used in science.名词词组+关系分句：

There is thermostat(恒温器)that controls the temperature. There is aplace in the valley where we always camp. There is no mother but loves her children (=who does not love her children). There is a strange old man who is smoking in the comer.注意，在非正式语体中，引导此类分句作主语的关系代词可省：

There's something(that)keeps up setting him to chop wood. There's a man at the door(who) wants to speak to you.正因为存在句的句尾可能出现上述复杂情况，它的意义有时也就变得复杂费解，如何分析理解这类问题须厘清句子的语法结构。这就是“复杂存在句”(Complex Existential Sentence)。例如：

There are few experiences that compare with the view of golden sand and green-blue water of Australia's most famous coast—Gold Coast.(没有什么经历比得上观赏澳大利亚著名黄金海岸金沙碧海之怡人美景。) There are also tips on how parents can help children remember numbers and words through various games, such as giving the child one word made of two, like mailbox, and asking them to reverse the two parts, so it becomes boxmail.(父母亲怎样通过游戏帮助孩子认数字，学单词也是有窍门的，比如给孩子一个由两部合成的词如“mailbox”,然后再叫孩子把两部合成的顺序颠倒过来，就变成了“boxmail”。) There are no scientists in existence that really wait until they have all the evidence in front of them before they try to work out what it might possibly mean.(没有哪个现存的科学家会真的等到所有的证据都摆在他面前之后才去设法找出问题的答案。) 334第29讲

There should be no question in the mind of any student who has shown any interest in the subjects he is leaming that his mastery of any subject is painstaking and time-consuming.(任何学生只要对他攻读的学科真感兴趣，都会认识到熟练掌握任何一门功课都需要刻苦的努力和花费大量的时间，这是毋庸置疑的。) To make too cheap and sell too high, there are two requirements, one of which is that you must have a lot of consumers with surplus money and unlimited wants, and the other of which is that the market for labor and new materials should remain depressed relative to the market for retail commodities.(要做到低成本和高售价，必须具备两个条件：第一是你必须有许多消费者，他们拥有过剩的购买力和无限的购买欲；第二是人力市场和原料市场必须保持比零售市场相对低廉的价位。) There is some useful experience of other countries from which we can draw reference and the essence of which, if adopted wisely, based on the conclusion in his report, can become a shortcut to the fulfillment of our goals.(其他国家有一些好经验可供我们参考并吸取其精华；别国的经验，只要善于学习，根据他报告中的结论，就能为实现我们的目标找到一条捷径。)

### EXERCISE 29A

Turn the following into existential sentences: 1. In the mountain we found an old man living in a cave. 2. During tomorrow's show we will have an interval of 15minutes between two items. 3.Yesterday we didn't have any difficulty finding the restaurant. 4. It seems that my decision is somewhere wrong. 5. If this motion is passed, we are sure to meet with a lot of opposition from the working class. 6. Our library keeps at least one book about the travels of Marco Polo. 7. Several trains were coming into the station at the same time. 8. A good film will be on at the Rex next week. 9. It is certain that the factory has some trouble with the workers. 10. Well, Mr.Green,the heating system doesn't seem to have anything wrong. We'd better look at the pump. 11.People believed that an ancient city lay somewhere in the mountains. 12. Beneath our house was an old cellar with a thick flint wall. 13. A boy named George Sampeter sat in the same class as me. 14.Monkeys were under the trees, swinging from the branches,everywhere. 15. A strange caller yesterday wouldn't say what his business was. 16. It's likely there will be a large audience. 17. We should have a stronger light on the staircase. 18. I'm sure that a mistake has been made. 19. The Maldives(马尔代夫群岛)consist of several thousand little islands. 20.My front door had a bolt but I never used it. 21. Getting a visa won't be difficult. 22. More than a million species of insects exist in the world. 23. Two more suspects are thought to be under arrest. 24. They are bound to react badly to the news. 25. A statement by the prime minister now follows. 26. From the middle of the forest emerged a strange hooded figure.

27. In this case securing a conviction should be no trouble. 28. Is nobody here able to help us? 29. Hanging over the bed was a beautiful tapestry. 30. People think at least two politicians are involved in the scandal.

## 29.2 存在句的非限定形式及其句法功能

存在句的谓语动词也可采取非限定形式，即there to be或 there being形式，这就是非限定存在句(Non-finite Existential Sentence),其句法功能如下。

1)作主语先说 there being形式。there being形式作主语只能置于句首。例如：

There being abus stop so near the house is a great advantage. There not being an index(索引)to this book is a disadvantage.再说 there to be形式。作主语时，必须用 for there to be⋯: For there to be so few people in the streets was unusual. For there to be a mistake ina computer's arithmetic(运算)is impossible.也可以在句首采用it作形式主语而把 for there to be...移至句尾：

It was unusual for there to be no latecomers. It would be surprising for there not to be any objections. 2)作宾语能接 there to be形式作宾语的及物动词不多。例如：

We expect there to be no argument. I want there to be no mistake about this. Bill intended there to be a meeting afterwards. The architect(建筑师)meant there to be another storeyadded later. Members like there to be plenty of choice. Housewives hate there to be queues everywhere. I'd prefer there to be a little more co-operation. We do not want there to be any disturbance.在某些只能跟-ing分词、不能跟 to-不定式的及物动词后，要用there being形式：

The committee anticipated there being less justification(申辩)in the second case. 3)作介词补足成分如果介词是for,则用 there to be形式：

The secretary arranged for there to be another interview. They planned for there to be a family reunion., Brian was waiting for there to be complete silence. Jim asked for there to be give-and-take(互让)on both sides.如果介词不是for,则用there being形式：

Bill was relying on there being another opportunity. We were annoyed at there being so much time wasted. There is now some hope of there being a settlement of the dispute. He asked about there being another meeting. 336 第29讲

Mary did not agree about there being no need for mothers to go out to work. Eleanor did not like the idea of there being nobody to talk to. Were you disappointed at there not having been more applause? We were surprised at there being such alarge deputation(代表团)to meet us. We have no objection to there being a meeting here, so long as it is peaceful. 4)作状语there being形式也能作状语。例如：

There being no further business, the chairman closed the meeting at 12:30. There having been no rain fora long time, the ground was burst black by the sun.上述用法即通常所谓的“独立结构”,仅限于正式语体，在一般场合通常说：

As there was no further business, the chairman closed the meeting at 12:30. As there had been no rain fora long time, the ground was burnt black by the sun.

## 29.3 存在句在语篇中的应用

如前所述，存在句的主要交际功能是传达新信息，引出新话题。这种句型之所以要用 there作形式主语而把“实义主语”置于动词之后正是为了让这种“实义主语”处于述位(Rheme),成为信息中心，以达到引出新话题之目的。正由于存在句具有引出新话题的特殊交际功能，所以常在语篇中被用来作主题句，借以介绍一个人或物，随即对之作进一步的说明。例如：

Too Many Cats Manyyears ago, there was an old woman. She did not like children at all. But she loved cats. She had black cats and white cats. She had mother cats and baby cats. So the children all came to her house. They came to play with the cats. More and more cats came to the old woman's house. Soon there were too many cats. The old woman couldn't feed them all. Then she had an idea."The children love my cats," she thought. So she gave each child a cat. Then she was happy. And the children were happy. And the cats were happy, too, because they each had a home. (Helen Fritch)又例如：

Required class attendance is so common at colleges and universities that many teachers and even students themselves simply assume it as a good thing. But in fact, required class attendance is not an effective way to motivate learning. There are several reasons why teachers should not require attendance. First, rigid attendance policy does not benefit students. For example, a student may have to give up a lecture he finds vital to his studies simply because he dare not risk being penalized for skipping class occasionally. Second, the lectures of some teachers are not always constructive. When a teacher merely repeats material from the textbook or wastes class time telling irrelevant stories, a student should be allowed to decide for himself whether an in-class experience is worthwhile. Finally and

most importantly, there is no proven correlation between attendance and performance. A student may never miss a single lecture, but who knows, he may be just sitting there daydreaming all the time. Required class attendance may secure one hundred percent attendance for a course, but it does not make much sense.

Choose between THERE TO BE and THERE BEING: 1. The army doesn't anticipate______much opposition from the rebel forces. 2. The ministry didn't expect___quite such a negative reaction from farmers. 3. We don't envisage____any adverse reaction from the viewers. 4.______not a hospital near the house is a great disadvantage. 5.People desire_______law and order all the year round. 6.______no further business, I went home. 7. We hate to see____disturbances everywhere in the country. 8. They are making arrangements for_________another conference. 9. We do not want___ another war on the border. 10. We were annoyed at ___so much money wasted. 11. It is essential for. more houses built next year. 12. People hope. a settlement of the dispute. 13. People hate_ queues everywhere in the country. 14. I asked for__ another interview. 15. What he wanted was____a reunion of the family.

Transform the following into existential sentences or the finite existential clauses into the non-finite: 1. An impregnable castle(坚固的城堡)stood on the top of the hill. 2. A time will come when you look back on this day with pride. 3. Mary didn't agree that there was no need for mothers to go out to work.(BEGIN: Mary didn't agree about ...) 4. Were you disappointed because there had not been more applause?(BEGIN:Were you disappointed at...) 5. John asked that there should be give-and-take(让步)on both sides. 6. A cow was crossing the road. 7. A plant needs light and water. 8. We do not want any disturbance to occur. 9. There is an index to this book.It is a great advantage. 10. I'm surprised there isn't an index. 11. We have no objection to anyone holding a meeting here. 12. What's impossible is that there should be a mistake in a computer's arithmetic(计算机的运算).(BEGIN: It is impossible for ...) 338 第29讲

13. What's not uncommon is that there should be problems of communication between old and young. (BEGIN:It is not uncommon for...) 14. An important message is just being decoded(译出). 15. Quite a number of very valuable jewels were stolen. 16. It was expected that there would be more discussion.(BEGIN: There...) 17. It is believed that there is/are a number of wounded on both sides. 18. A meeting about the new building took place yesterday. 19. Three accidents have occurred at this crossroads this week. 20. A lawn extends from the house to the river. 21.People say there is trouble on the border.(BEGIN: There...) 22. A year ago there was trouble about it, which I can just remember.(BEGIN: I can just remember the trout ble...) 23. Some people will be anxious that the plan should succeed. 24. # 第30讲 IT-句型和否定结构

30.1 IT-句型IT-句型指用“非指代性it”(Non-referring it)作形式主语或形式宾语的句式。所谓“非指代性it”指不带“先行项”(Antecedent)的it,这就是说它不同于单数第三人称中性指代性 it,它仅在句中作形式主语或形式宾语。

1)It在表示时间、距离等句式中作形式主语非指代性it用于此类句式可表示多种意义，比如表示时间、日期：

What time is it?It's ten o'clock. What date is it? It's December 10th. It is six years since he left. It will be a long time before we see him again.表示天气、温度：

It's stormy out at sea. It was snowing last night. It can be very warm in September. It's high tide/low tide. It's full moon tonight. In winter it's dark at six o'clock.表示距离：

How far is it to York?It is 400 kilometres. It is not far to the nearest village. It is a long way to downtown Manhattan(曼哈顿商业区). It is only three miles to the airport.表示笼统情况：

It's awful—I have so much work to do. It has fared well with him(一切顺利). It was dull when Mary was away. 340 第30讲

It was amusing when the conjuror(魔术师)produced(变出)the rabbit. How is it(=your life, work, etc) going? Isn'tit lovely here? 2)用作先行主语每当句子的实义主语为某些非限定分句或其他名词性分句时，通常是把实义主语后移，而代之以先行it作形式主语。例如：

It's nice to be with you. It's difficult to understand what she's talking about. It took me three hours to get home last night. Will t suit you to come tomorrow evening? It's no use trying to explain—I'm not interested. Is it/there any use trying to phone him? It's crazy her going off like that. It's not much good expecting Andrew to help. It's important that everybody should be told all the facts. It seems obvious that we can't go on like this. It shocked me that Peter didn't tell anybody where he was. It occurred to me that she might have forgotten the date. It doesn't interest me whether you succeed or not. It doesn't matter when you arrive—just come when you can. It's surprising how many unhappy marriages there are. It wasn't very clear what she meant. 3)用于“It seemslappears/looks/happens+that-分句”等句式It seems that no one has any objection to the idea. It seems(that)he was late for the train. It seems as though our plan'll be perfect. It appeared that he had a taste for music. It appears to me that this novel is not true to life. It looks as if it will be a nice day. It looks as if we shall have to walk home. It happens that his sister is a close friend of mine. It happened that the harvest was bad that year. It turns out that he was never there. It turned out that his “country cottage”was an enormous bungalow(平房). 4)用于“It depends..., It's time...”之类的句式It depends how you tackle the problem.(那就看你怎样处理这个问题了。) It depends(on) whether you want to do it or not.(那就看你愿不愿做了。) It's time to start. It's time for us to go. It's time that we had a rest. It's time we were leaving. It's high time we left.

5)用于分裂句作形式主语It was my aunt who took Peter to London,not my mother. It was Peter that my aunt took to London, not Lucy.关于分裂句，详见39.4。

6)非指代性it作先行宾语I find it difficult to talk to you. My blister made it a problem to walk. I thought it strange that she hadn't written. George made it clear what he wanted. I thought it awaste of money to throw away the food. She found it difficult to hear what he said. He made it a rule to take a long walk in the afternoon. He felt it his duty to mention the fact to her. I think it very unwise going on as we are without definite agreements. Officials have said they believe it unlikely that any lasting damage to the environment has been done. I think it best that you should stay here. He soon made it clear why he had asked for a conference. used to feel it a terrible thing that my mother should have to toil so endlessly. 7)非指代性 it用于某些习语中作形式宾语“非指代性it”也常用在某些习语中作形式宾语，对这些习语，只能从整体上理解，it完全没有意义。例如：

You may take it from me(相信我的话):your troubles are nearly over. Go it(拼命干)while you are young. The ayes have it.(投票赞成的占多数。) You'll catch it(受责备)when your father finds that you have been trampling over the flower beds. Don't leave us; stick it out(坚持到底)a few years more. Asilluck would have it(不巧),he was not at home when l called. Climbing the mountain on the rainy day, he had a hard time of it(吃苦). He seldom speaks, but when he opens his mouth, he puts his foot in it(说错话). I'm afraid we've had it(没有希望了/倒了霉了):the last bus left five minutes ago. No nation should lord it over(对⋯⋯称王称霸)other nations. He was hard put to it(难以(做某事))to decide whether to stay or to quit. If we start early we'll be able to make it(来得及/及时到达). They quarrelled but soon made it up(和好)again. The boys will now make ithot for him(弄得他日子不好过). If you're going to change your clothes, you'd better make it snappy(赶快).We'll have to leave at any moment. We decided to go swimming at midnight just for the hell of it(只是为了开心). You can'tget away with it(逍遥法外). Have you got it(弄懂)at last? A few minutes after the burglars left, she got it all together(镇定下来，恢复镇定)and called the police. Small businessmen usually get it in the neck(遭殃)inan economic crisis. 342 第30讲

There's no help forit(无法可想，没有法子). With the bridge destroyed, there was nothing for it(无法可想);we had to swim across. There's nothing for it but(除⋯⋯别无他法)extraction;that tooth must come out. We are going to rough it(过艰苦生活)on this trip. It's I who catch it(受责，挨骂)every time anything goes wrong. It ake it(认为)that you wanted something else. I'm going to have it out with them(跟他们辩个明白)this afternoon. Take it easy(慢点开).The roads are icy. He is of a quick temper. Take it easy(沉住气)in arguing with him. Shall we walk it or cabin(步行去还是打的去)?由以上最后一例可以看出，it还可用在少数几个由名词转化来的动词后面作形式宾语，这时it 也是完全没有意义的。例如：

There's no convenience; we must foot it. Shall we tram it or bus it home? They had to leg it back to camp. We therefore decided that we would sleep out(露宿)on fine nights, and hotel it and inn it and pub it when it was wet.上述关于it的这些用法都只见于非正式语体。

Do that again and you'll catch it. 4. If you go mountaineering in such weather, you'll have to rough it. 5. With the bridge destroyed, there was nothing for it;we had to swim across. 6. There's no help for it. 7. If you break the law, you can't get away with it. 8. We decided to go swimming at midnight just for the hell of it. 9. If we start early we'll be able to make it. 10. He was hard put to it to decide whether to stay or to quit. 11.I gave it to him hot. 12.Hop it. It's no time for chatting. The president took it on the lam in an American helicopter. They quarrelled but soon made it up again. 15. If you're going to change your clothes,youd better make it snappy.Well have to leave at any moment. 16.A few minutes after the burglar left, she got it all o gether and called the police. 17.Small businessmen usually get it in the neck in an economic crisis. 18.He seldom speaks,but whenever he does,he puts his foot in it. 19.No nation should lord it over other nations. 20. Rephrase the following sentences using anticipatory it: 1. Possibly the Joneses will come to the party as well. 2. Whether they will come or not doesn't matter very much. 3.He is quite likely to let you down. 4. You seem to have taken a dislike to him. 5.I happen to have had some nasty experiences in dealing with him. 6. Getting the car on the boat was rather complicated. 7. I think a good idea would be to get up a petition. 8. To be here with you this evening is a great pleasure. 9. You were so kind to invite us. 10. What you say does not matter in the least. 11. How he came to have such a valuable painting is a mystery. 12. That you should feel obliged to resign at this point is regretted. 13. To spoil such a fine drawing would be a pity. 14.Seeing you sitting here all alone makes me feel sad. 15. Do you find living here very dull? 16. I cannot possibly forgive him. 17.People didn't know whether there was gold left in the mine. 18. The park looks very small. 19. Our plan seems to be perfect. 20. He clearly indicated that he didn’t want to speak to me.

## 30.2 否定结构—

否定结构就是由否定词构成的语法结构。在系统介绍否定结构之前，我们首先要交代一下“肯定词”(Assertive Word)、“非肯定词”(Non-assertive Word)和否定词(Negative Word)这三类词语。这三种词语涉及限定词、代词、副词三个词类，而且肯定词大多有与之相对应的非肯定词和否定词。兹将除否定词not 以外的三类词语列表如下：

所属词类 肯定词 非肯定词 否定词限定词/代词 some any no, none代词 someone anyone no one代词 somebody anybody nobody代词 something anything nothing副词 somehow (in any way) (in no way)副词 somewhere anywhere nowhere副词 someplace anyplace no place副词 sometime(s) ever never副词 somewhat any (the) no,(none the)代词 one or the other either neither 344 第30讲

(续表)副词 still any more no more副词副词副词 already(to some extent)as well, too yetat alleitherany longer 一一no longer一一般说来，肯定词用于肯定句；否定词用于否定句，而非肯定词则用于否定句以及带有否定意向的疑问句。否定结构除用not,no 以及no-合成词外，还可用“半否定词”(Semi-negative)。半否定词包括barely,rarely,hardly, scarcely,seldom 以及带否定含义的限定词/不定代词 few/ little。在此类词语之后，须用非肯定词或肯定的附加问句。例如：

We barely had time to catch the train. The little boy could barely read and speak in English. I hardly ever go out these days. In those days I scarcely got any sleep. Rarely have seen such a beautiful sunset. Little does he care whether we live or die. Hardly anybody believes that, does he? Few people want to go there, do they? 1)否定范围否定范围(Scope of Negation)指否定意义在句中扩及的范围。一般说来，在否定词之后只要没有肯定词便都在否定范围以内。例如：

Nothing was found in the drawer. lam not interested in the fim at all.否定句中的句尾状语(分句)是否在否定范围以内，在书面语中须视状语之前是否有逗号：

I didn't go to the cinema because I had seen the film. I didn't go to the cinema, because I had seen the film.但在口语中须视调型而定：

It didn't rain all the month.(=For a whole month, it didn't rain.) It didn't(rain)"all the month.(=It did rain,but not for a whole month.)否定范围不仅可以延及句尾状语，有时还可能延及其他限定分句和非限定分句。例如：

Nobody has promised that any of you will be released yet. That wouldn't deter anyone who had any courage. I have not any doubt that he will succeed. lam not certain whetherhe will come. I wouldn't like you to disturb anyone.如前所述，在否定范围内，要用非肯定词。如果在否定词之后用了肯定词，那它就不在否定范围内，从而产生意义上的差别。试比较：

jl don't like any of the songs.(=I like none of the songs.) lI don't like some of the songs.(=There are some songs that I don't like.) Lydia hasn't eaten anything yet.(=Up to now Lydia hasn't eaten anything yet.){Lydia hasn't eaten anything for three days already.(=It's already three days that Lydia hasn't eaten anything.)但评注性状语和连接性状语，不论出现在什么位置上，总是在否定范围之外。例如：

We never found out the truth, unfortunately. She doesn'tknow him,however. Oddly enough,he hasn't said anything about it. George was very good at arithmetic.Yet he was never given full marks. 2)否定焦点否定焦点(Focus of Negation),是指在口语中表示对比含义的否定调核在句中的确切位置；此种调核的位置，往往引起否定范围被分隔，从而导致整个句子意义的改变。所谓否定范围被分隔，是指在否定词和被否定的词语之间存在着肯定的部分，本书用( )表示；有时，否定词之前的部分也可能包括在否定范围以内。试观察以下诸例：

[1]I didn't take Tom to swim in the pool today.[2] I didn't(take)Tom to swim in the pool today. ( )[3] I didn't(take Tom)to swim in the pool today. ( )[4] I didn't(take Tom to swim)in the pool today.( )_ _____[5]I didn't(take Tom to swim in the pool) to day. ( )_[6](') didn't take Tom to swim in the pool today.现在让我们来分析以上诸句，要注意句中“否定焦点”落在何处，这涉及“否定范围”被分隔的情况(其中被肯定的部分用( )表示)。

句[1]无对比含义，此句相当于：

I didn't/forgot to take Tom to swim in the pool today.句[2]的否定焦点落在Tom上，意思是：

I took someone to swim in the pool, but it wasn't Tom.句[3]的否定焦点落在swim上，意思是：

I took Tom to the pool today just to see it.句[4]的否定焦点落在pool上，意思是：

I took Tom to swim at the seaside,not in the pool.句[5]的否定焦点落在today 上，意思是：

I did take Tom to swim in the pool, but not today.句[6]的否定焦点落在1上，意思是：

It was my wife who took Tom to swim in the pool today.根据否定焦点也可判断否定范围是否扩及从属分句。比较：

[1]I didn't(buy the book) because I am interested in poetry. ()___=I bought the book, but it wasn't because I am interested in poetry.[2] I didn't buy the book(,)because I'm interested in poetry.=I didn't buy the book(,)because it has nothing to do with poetry.[3] She didn't(come to see him) when he asked. ( )_____=She came to see him, but not at the time he asked her to come.[4] She didn't come to see him when he asked.=When he asked,she didn't come to see him. 346 第30讲

在[1]、[3]两句中，否定焦点分别落在poetry和 asked上，主句与从句同属一个语调组，从而 because-分句和when-分句被包括在否定范围以内；在[2]、[4]两句中，主句与从句分属两个语调组，故从句不包括在否定范围以内。

3)转移否定转移否定(Transferred Negation)指按语义本应出现于从句的否定词not被移至主句的现象。例如：

I think(that) it's not going to rain today. I don't think(that)it's going to rain today.这两句话，意义相同，但其中否定词not的位置不同，尽管not在从句中的否定意义较强，却不如转移到主句普通，这种转移现象，在非正式语体中尤为常见。

能在主句中作转移否定的动词，按语义可分为两大类：

a)表示看法(opinion)的动词这类动词有anticipate, believe,calculate,expect, figure, imagine, reckon, suppose, think等等。例如：

She doesn't imagine we have finished the job yet.(她以为我们还没有完成任务。) We don't believe that he can do it.(我们相信他不能做此事。) I don't suppose that he will be back until eight.(我猜想他不会在8点以前回来。) We are not anticipating that there will be much trouble.(我们估计不会有多少麻烦。) Idon't expect Tom has eaten all the cake.(我料想汤姆没有吃完那块蛋糕。)由上述诸例可以看出，转移否定一般出现在含有限定从属分句(that-分句)的句子中，但有时也可出现在含有非限定分句的句子中。例如：

He didn't expect to go there tomorrow.(他料想明天不会去那里。)但是，并非所有表示看法的动词或其他结构都能用于转移否定。试观察下列各组句子中assume, fear,(1) am afraid等的用法：

jl don't assume that he came.(我不相信他来了。) assume that he didn't come.(我相信他没有来。) She doesn't fear that she may offend the boss.(她不怕得罪老板。) Ifear Parliament won't pass the bill.(我恐怕议会不会通过这项议案。) lam not afraid that he will come next week.(我不怕他下星期来。) lam afraid that he wil not come next week.(我恐怕他下星期不会来。)在上述三组句子中，每组第一句和第二句意义是不同的，因而每组第一句并不是转移否定，类似的动词还有 presume,surmise等。

b)表示认知(perception)的动词这类动词有appear, seem,feel as if,look as if,sound as if等。例如：

It doesn't seem that he's telling the truth.(看来他没有讲真话。) It doesn't sound as if they know what to do.(听上去好像他们不知道该做什么。) It doesn't look like it's going to rain.(看来不会下雨。)最后，还要指出一点，并非所有“I don't think+ that-分句”结构都一定是“否定前移”即转移否定现象。例如：

I don't think Mary will come tomorrow.这句话在think 不受重读的情况下，语义重心自然落在that-分句上，这时否定范围便包括了that-分句，从而在意义上相当于I think Mary will not come tomorrow,只是用I don't think ...结构时，语势较弱。

另一方面，如果think 受重读，语义重心落在think 上，其否定范围便只限于主句，从而不存在转移否定，整句的意义也就不同了。比如说：

I don't 'think she will come tomorrow,I 'know she will.(我不是认为她明天会来，我知道她会来。)

4)双重否定双重否定(Double Negation)指一个词组里或分句里用了两个否定词的现象。此种否定结构往往起含蓄的强调作用。比如不说某人“很能干”,而说not incapable;不说“有所准备”,而说 not unprepared;不说“很有可能”,而说not impossible;不说“很经常”,而说not infrequently。例如：

His work is not inferior to mine.(= His work is just as well done as mine.) He spent no small amount of money on travelling. (= He spent a great deal of money on travelling.) He was not indifferent to political questions.(=He took a great interest in political questions.) He was none too kind to us.(=He was rather unkind to us.) He is not half a bad fellow.(=He is by no means a bad fellow/He is a good man.) I was not a little woried.(=I was very much worried.)以上诸例中的双重否定现象都存在于词组里，此种否定又叫做局部否定(Local Negation)。但是，双重否定往往并不限于词组里，它也常见于分句中，起到负负得正的作用。例如：

No one, surely, has never experienced such temptation.(= Everyone, surely, has at some time experienced such temptation.) Neither investigator had no financial interest in the company.(= Both investigators had some financial interest in the company.) Not all imperatives have no subject.(=Some imperatives have a subject.) Never before had none of the committee members supported the mayor.(= Some of the committee members had always supported the mayor.) 5)多余否定多余否定(Pleonastic/ Superfluous Negation)指在特定上下文中，用了否定词却不起否定作用的语法现象。例如：

I don't know whether he will not accept my proposal. It's going to rain; I wonder whether Mr Hunter will not come tonight. I'm going to search the office to see ifl could not find the missing document. I'm not sure whether she cannot speak French.

### EXERCISE 30C

Correct errors in the following sentences(in the case of pleonastic negation, correct them according to the requirement of standard English): 1. He found her inattractive but strangely appealing. 2. Sending a man to prison for defending national property is generally regarded as no justice. 3. The new Finance Director is detested by people. He was generally thought to be honest. 4. He says he doesn't know nothing about the robbery. 5. Antique watches of this type are not seen on the market. They are unavailable. 6. That kind of crime doesn’'t rarely happen,these days, does it? 7. None of the twin brothers want to be an actor. 8. Having seen the film,I don't understand the hype. 9. The boss ordered his men don't leave the room. 10. They haven't booked neither to go on a cruise nor to visit the Pyramids. 348 第30讲

11. Jenice has had only a few lessons on the guitar,so it seems likely that she has become a great guitarist. 12. A great number of songbirds are seen these days owing to the erosion of their natural habitats. 13. We hope that our soldiers meet with a lot of resistence when they enter the city. 14. I don’'t think the conference to be a failure. 15. If the payment is forthcoming, we will be obliged to cut your connection to the electricity. 16. I believe Phil is innocent.He has claimed all the allegations against him. 17. The director hated the investigation; he deliberately gave the investigators correct information. 18. Our company is on the brink of bankruptcy. I think it will be offering aid to the disaster zone this time. 19. The new twin-turbo engine generates a lot of noise; I think it is especially powerful. 20. I was willing to help the children as they had to take the responsibility for the schoolwork they brought home. 21. In this modern city you will get a lot of impression of life in the pre-capitalist era. 22. The estimated fee for the project is wildly unreasonable; we will soon be able to conclude the bargain. 23. This type of video film is really hard to understand.It is possible for us to catch its true colours. 24. Such war crimes could be accepted even in times of conflict. 25. As the problems about politics and religion are very hard to negotiate, it is best to talk about them on the first date.

# 第31讲 并列结构

## 31.1并列结构和并列句

1)并列结构的类型两个或两个以上意义相关、层次相同、句法功能也相同、并由并列连词(Coordinator)或其他并列手段连接起来的语法结构序列叫做“并列结构”(Coordinate Structure)。

并列结构分为不同的层次，它可以是词和词的并列：

knife and fork buy or sell good but expensive也可以是词组和词组的并列：

a teacher and a student one way or the other with strength but without confidence也可以是分句和分句的并列：由两个或两个以上并列分句构成的句子叫做并列句(Compound Sentence)。

例如：

The teacher's name is Smith, and the student's name (is)Keith. The children can go with us or they can stay at home. This sentence may be correct grammatically, but a native speaker never says it that way. Mary is a nurse, her brother is a doctor,and her parents are both famous writers.一串平行的句子，不带并列连词，但语义相关，结构相同，也可归入并列句之列。例如：

We were discussing where to go for the holiday: some wanted to go to the seaside; some liked to visit the “Sea World”;others prefered to stay ovemight in a country house.此类并列结构叫做“不带连词的并列结构”(Asyndetic Coordination);那常见的并列结构便叫“带连词的并列结构”(Syndetic Coordination)。

2)并列结构和标点符号由上述诸例可以看出，由两个以上项目构成的并列结构通常只在最后一个项目之前用并列连词，其他350 第31讲

项目之间仅用逗号连接。又例如：

There are five types of discharge from military service(退役):honorable, general, undesirable, bad conduct,and dishonorable. At diploma mills(文凭制造厂)throughout the states you can buy a bachelor's degree(学士学位) for about $1,500,amaster's degree for $1,600,ora doctorate for $1,700.但也有全用连词的。两个以上项目全用连词连接起来的并列结构，在修辞色彩上给人以悠闲缓慢、延续持久的感觉。例如：

Raising vegetables presents endless opportunities for weeding and thinning and hoeing and watering.相反地，有时也可全用逗号，这就在修辞色彩上给人以紧张、急促、明快、利落的感觉。例如：

Individuals are less troubled by feelings of guilt when they share responsibility for killings with a group-astreet gang,lynch mob(对黑人施私刑的暴民),terrorist organization.有时，在一个句子中有两个并列结构，其一全用逗号，另一全用连词，这样处理是为了达到上述两种修辞效果。例如：

In the country there are no honking homs(汽车喇叭声),no diesel trucks, no pollution—just the sounds of wind rustling the tree leaves and red squirrels(松鼠) chattering in the distant oaks and cool creek water pushing down its endless course.除了逗号，分号(;)和冒号(:)也能起并列连词的作用。一般说来，用分号连接两个并列分句，通常表示一种对比关系。例如：

Golf demands the best of time and space; tennis, the best of personal energy. After her father's death, she went out very little; after her sweetheart went away, people saw her hardly at all. He was caught:behind him was a locked door;in front of him were the officers and the dogs.在某些连接性状语(Conjunct)如 accordingly,furthermore, hence, however,nevertheless, therefore等之前通常要用分号，而不用逗号，因为这些词项是副词，而不是连词。例如：

He had worked in the foreign service for two years without leave; hence he was tired almost beyond endurance. From a running start Pedro launched his body into a vigorous racing dive;however, he was about thirty feet from the pool at the time. Nations indeed seem to have a kind of biological span like human life, from rebellious youth, through caution,to decay;consequently,predictions of doom are not uncommon.用冒号连接两个并列分句，通常表示下文是对上文的解释和发挥。例如：

A house with an aging furnace costs more than the asking price suggests:ten dollars more a month in fuel means about eighty dollars more a year. Hijackers and kidnappers offer two equally impossible alternatives: a refusal to meet their terms may mean death for their victims; meeting their terms may mean more victims in the future. 3)并列结构中的插入语不论是由两个或两个以上项目构成的并列结构，都可在最后一个项目之前插入一个词语，从而使这个项目处于更加突出的地位。例如：

The conference on nuclear energy was attended by students from Stanford and,predictably, teachers from Berkeley. Donna was happy with her achievement but, after three days of intense competition, tired from the struggle. Keith spends Saturday night walking down the street or,when the cops are after him, running through alleys.

John Frankenheimer has directed action movies(导演动作片)such as "The Manchurian Candidate”,"Seven Days in May",“Grand Prix”,and, more recently, “Black Sunday and Brinks”. 4)并列结构的对称组合如果一个并列结构由四个或四个以上项目构成，常用成对组合的办法使整个结构更加紧凑。例如：

Only a few cities are known as “good baseball towns”—Cincinnati, Detroit, Chicago, Boston, Los Angeles, and New York.上述六个并列项目可以重新组合为三个平行的并列结构：

Only a few cities are known as “good baseball towns”— Cincinnati and Detroit, Chicago and Boston, Los Angeles and New York.又例如：

Black American writers like Frederick Douglas, W.E.B. Du Bois, Malcolm×,and Eldridge Cleaver have found autobiography(自传) an especially congenial(宜人的)form.以上并列结构可作如下的重新组合：

Black American writers from Frederick Douglas and W.E. B. Du Bois to Malcolm×and Eldridge Cleaver have found autobiography an especially congenial form. 5)并列句的类型以上4节介绍了并列结构的形式和意义，其中许多方面都适用于并列句。本节再专一把并列句的类型梳理一下。

并列句就是由两个或两个以上并列分句通过各种并列手段连接起来的句式。连接并列分句的手段可以是并列连词，可以是连接副词，也可以是某些标点符号如分号和冒号。例如带并列连词的并列句：

The signal was given, and the ship moved slowly out of the dock. Being idle sometimes is agreeable,but being idle all the time might become monotonous. We must be early or(else) we won't get a seat. When I saw her in the river Iwas frightened,for at that point the currents were dangerous. We must run, otherwise we will be too late.带连接副词的并列句：

When I got to the pub, I had a drink; then l went home. He was angry;nevertheless, he listened to me. He says he is a scholar;however he has three houses and drives a Rolls. lonly have an old car;stil i's better than nothing. lam busy today;so can you come to my place tomorrow? I've advised her to be modest time and again;yet she's never apologized for anything in her life. I can't go now.I'm too busy;besides/moreover,my passport is out of date. Nancy had lived with the man for a long time; therefore she was unable to avoid an unwelcome marriage.带分号和冒号的并列句；He didn't go downtown; he went swimming. Jil fetches water every day;Jack accompanies her only on Sundays. The student became somewhat confused;he could not understand what it meant. To err is human; to blame it on the other party is politics(手腕). Work when you work;play when you play. This was no “stitch in time”;it was complete reconstruction.(这并不是“及时修补”,而是彻底重建。) 352 第31讲

We decided not to go on holiday:we had too little money. Mother may have to go into hospital:she's got kidney trouble. The results of the poll(民意测验)were surprising: seven percent in favour, eleven percent opposed, eighty-two percent no opinion. You can't count on him to help:he is such a selfish man. Bob was caught:he was accused of corruption. This year's wheat harvest is poor: there has been no rain for three months. She didn't listen to me:she never apologized for anything in her life.下面一则练习要求将每一组句子改写成一个含有并列结构的句子。

### EXERCISE 31A

Combine each group of sentences into one sentence, using coordination: 1. Brian locked the door. He went to a nearby pub. 2. Tom speaks Chinese fluently. He can't write in it. 3. The students study current events on Friday afternoon. If there is nothing special to study, they have League meetings. 4. Ruel went into the forest. Cameron went into the forest too. They collected a lot of insect specimens(昆虫标本). 5. On his way up the hill ack fell down. He hurt his hands and knees. Jimmy got to the top all right. 6. The girls decided to remain at home.It was raining hard. 7. They could not decide what to do.They asked my advice. 8. For the first time I saw my father not as the giant of my childhood. I saw my father simply as a lonely man. 9. Fewer blacks are leaving the rural South these days. They are staying not because farming has become more popular.They are staying because industry is moving in. 10. The four largest hotel companies in the United States are Hilton, Sheraton, Hyatt, and Western Interna-tional. Western International is the fastest growing hotel company. 11. No matter how you measure it, education is the largest "industry" in the nation.You can measure it in terms of dollars spent.You can measure it in terms of people involved. 12. There is concision(简洁)in these lines.There is elegance in them too. 13. Mr Fisher does not love the environment. He is not accustomed to the weather either. 14. Television has been called in the United States a source of information. It is also called a means of entertainment. Its severest critics call it a “plug-in”drug(电插麻醉药). 15.For decades FBI agents(联邦调查局侦探)wiretapped(搭线窃听)the phones of American citizens. They did so with the attorney general's approval. Yet they did not have warrants(授权书).下面一则练习要求在空白处填入适当标点符号，用逗号，还是用分号或冒号，还是不用标点符号。

In the following sentences, determine appropriate punctuation to be used in the places marked by brackets. Would you use commas, semicolons, colons, or no punctuation at all? 1. His hair was white and stood up wildly on his head( )nevertheless I was struck by a singular neatness in his appearance. 2. It was due,I suppose( )to his lofty stature and immaculate dress( )no doubt he has a careful wife looking after him. 3. Like all distinguished men in political life( )he spoke with assurance, even with arrogance( )yet I could not help sensing a touch of anxiety in his behavior. 4. I walked up to him then( )and stretched out my hand( )but he evidently failed to recognize me. 5. When a man has the weight of nations on his shoulders( )he may be forgiven for overlooking indi-viduals( )but I confess I was angry. 6. It is one thing to be dignified and detached( )it is quite another to be downright rude. 7. I had arrived early( )as was my habit( )I, therefore,felt privileged to take my leave without delay. 8. The affair was not the worst I have ever endured( )but it was nearly so( )at such times one wishes one could escape at any cost. 9. Once I had arrived at the entrance( )however( )there was no turning back. 10. When I go to a place like that( )I go gladly( )when I return( )I come home even more gladly. 11. Economic theorists assume a common man( )he commonly wants more than he can supply. 12. John demands the most from himself( )Pete from others. 13. His game was ragged( )he went into sand traps four times,and into the trees five. 14. The country wants the new expressway( )the city wants to renew its streets. 15. Sports at any age are beneficial( )they keep your pulses hopping.

## 31.2并列连词—

英语有三个主要的并列连词(Coordinator),即and, or, but。还有三个词项nor,so,yet,它们既可作并列连词，也可作连接性副词。此外还有几对关联并列连词(Correlative Coordinator),它们是both⋯and,either... or,not...but, not...nor, neither...nor, not only ... but also。还有几个近似并列连词(Quasi-coordinator),如as well as,as much as,rather than, more than等，它们是介乎并列连词与从属连词(Subordinator)或复杂介词(Complex Preposition)之间的语法结构，这就是“边际并列连词”。

最后还有一个 for,也是介乎并列连词与从属连词之间的，为了能与 because的用法作比较说明，本书把它放在下一讲从属结构中处理。

按词汇意义划分，英语并列连词可分为三类：以and为代表的表示语义引申的并列连词；以or为代表的表示选择的并列连词；以but为代表的表示转折和对比的并列连词。分别介绍如下：

1)以and为代表的表示语义引申的并列连词这类并列连词包括 and,both ...and,not only...but (also),not.⋯.nor,neither ... nor等。这一类并列连词在语义上表示其所连接的成分是对前项的补充和引申，包括肯定和否定两种意义的引申。例如：

As is reported, a trade agreement was signed, and a cultural exchange was arranged. Mrs Cox is both kind and gentle to her pupils. 354 第31讲

Not only is he himself interested in the subject, but all his students are beginning to show an interest in it. Alf is not going to the movies, noraml. Neither has Pauline wanted to rent a house,nor has her husband.应该指出，由and连接并列成分除表示语义增补以外，还可表示其他意义。例如：

He is Jack of all trades and master of none.(表示转折) He opened the door and went in.(表示顺序) He heard a cry for help,and (therefore)he rushed out of the house.(表示因果) Think it over again and you'll ind away out.(表示结果) Mary likes music and Jim is fond of sports.(表示对比) He has a somewhat swelled head(骄傲自大),and I don't like this.(表示评注)还有一些由and连接的语法结构形似并列，实际并非并列结构。例如：

Why don't you come and have dinner with us?(=Why don't you come to have dinner with us?) This room is nice and wam.(=nicely warm) I'll go and bring back your boots.(=go in order to bring back) You must try and come to the party.(=try to come) Can you touch pitch and not be defiled?(=without being defiled) 2)以or为代表的表示选择的并列连词这类并列连词包括or和either...or。例如：

You have to do it one way or the other. The workers were cheerful, or at least they appeared to be cheerful. Either youorlmust do it. There are only two possibilities: either you know or you don't know.除表示选择外，or和either...or还可表示否定的条件。例如：

Make up your mind,or(=if not)you'l miss the chance. You'll either behave yourself, or you'l never go out with me.由上述诸例可以看出，not only.... but (also),neither... nor,either ... or均可分别位于两个分句句首构成并列句，而both...and却不可以这样用。比如可以说：

Not only did the garage overcharge me, but they hadn't done a very good repair job either. Either the president will resign through ill health, or he will die in office. Neither has he called on her,nor will he do so.却不可以说：

* Both Jane wanted to go to the south, and her husband wanted to go there,too.还必须指出，neither ... nor/ either...or 在用法上与both ... and/not onl.... but (also)也有区别。

当neither/either 出现在主动词之前时，随后的nor / or 既可带主动词，也可不带主动词，而意义无异。

例如：

He neither likes fiction nor(likes)poetry. He has either gone to the movies or(gone) to the theatre.有时neither/either所引导的是述谓成分(Predication),而nor/or却能引导整个的分句。例如：

①try and与go and的用法相似，但又有所区别。go and可用于一切时式，而try and却不能用于过去时。例如可以说：

He went and bought another one.却不可以说：

* He tried and stopped me.

He neither likes fiction,nor does he like poetry. He has either gone to the movies, or he has gone to the theatre.而both ...and/ not only...but (also)却通常不可以这样用。比如不可以说：

× He both likes fiction and poetry.× He not only likes fiction but also poetry.也不可以说：

× He both likes fiction and he likes poetry. x He not only likes fiction but he also likes poetry. 3)以but为代表的表示语义转折和对比的并列连词这类并列连词包括but, not..but, while,whereas, only,yet等。例如：

The young man has often been praised, but he is never conceited. He said that he was busy today but that he would be free tomorrow. This coat is not mine but yours. It never rains but it pours. Jane is slender,while Mary is stout. My children want to live in town, whereas l myself would rather live in the country. He very much wanted to buy the house, only he could not afford it. She's a funny girl,yet you can't help liking her.下面做一则改错练习。

### EXERCISE 31C

Correct errors, if any,in the following sentences: 1. My summer's work proved not only interesting but also I learned much from it. 2.A college education was both necessary and I could afford it. 3. Both Mary will do it and her husband will do it too. 4. You can either write a thesis or a short essay on the subject. 5. Her lectures are witty, interesting, and she outlines them carefully. 6.Macy is a middle-aged woman,short, stocky, blue eyes,and partly with gray hairs. 7、She told us to read the text carefully, and that we should write a short review of it. 8. Either he is an absolute piker(胆小鬼)or a fool. 9. He not only likes the girl, but the family too. 10. The students are not only organizing social activities, but also are interested in political questions. 11. One should take care of ones physical self as well as being able to read and write. 12. He can neither sing nor he can compose(作曲). 13. Either he needs some rest or some cold drink. 14. Both Pauline and her husband did not want to attend the meeting. 15. Donna was satisfied not with her own achievement or with the team's performance. 16. The boy does not smoke,not drink alcohol,and not idle away his time. 17.Gas is a substance without a definite volume nor with a definite shape. 18. We were told to write in ink, that we should use but one side of the paper,and we should endorse(在⋯⋯上签名)our papers in the proper manner. 356 第31讲

19.New Hampshire is the only state without a sales tax(销售税)or an income tax,a major attraction for tax-weary residents of other states. 20. The admen(广告员)not only convince the reader that the Continental is a luxury car but also that the car confers status on its owner. 21. Give him an inch,or he'll take a mile. 22:You should have tried or finished the paper by Saturday. 23. Mr Jones is the principal of the school, moreover he is an expert in translation. 24. As soon as he arrived in town, he went seeing the doctor immediately. 25. Just take a look at that fellow,so you'll get sick.

## 31.3并列与排比

1)什么是排比排比(Parallelism)是一种作为修辞手法的并列结构，它是由两个、三个或者三个以上的同类语法结构成串排列，以达到结构匀称、说理有力、叙事生动、抒情深刻的修辞效果。例如：

Tom is a strapping(魁梧的)young man,big-boned and big-muscled. He is tall, dark, and lean. There is no debt, no benefit,no burden,no obligation in his case. He pulled his head back into the bedroom, looked around, saw his jacket, picked it up, and put it on. We were all afraid of the gobbling Miss B:she spied, she pried, she crouched, she crept, she pounced-she was a terror.(我们都害怕那位张牙舞爪的B小姐：她暗中监视，她刺探隐私，她卑躬屈膝，她阿谀奉承——她真令人生畏。) 2)排比的句法功能排比结构能在句中作多种成分，比如作主语：

The well-decorated roomy hall, the melodious music and the friendly atmosphere are sure to put you in the mood fora ball.(宽敞华丽的大厅、悠扬悦耳的音乐和友好的气氛一定会使你神怡心荡，急欲参加舞会。) Household appliances, home delivery, the Internet, 24-hour shopping, and more varied and affordable domestic services have increased flexibility and freed up people's time.(家用电器、送货上门、因特网、24小时购物、多样化的、廉价的家政服务已经使人们能够灵活地自由地支配时间。) When the evening closed in, the difficulty and danger of walking along London became serious indeed. The garret windows were opened, and pails were emptied, with little regard to those who were passing below. Falls, bruises, and broken bones were constant occurrences. (Macaulay)(夜幕降临，在伦敦街头走路真是问题严重(指几百年前的伦敦——编者),阁楼窗户打开了，整桶污水倾倒下来，毫不顾及过往行人。摔跤、跌伤、骨折之类的事故屡屡发生。)作宾语：

They think women, in gaining their freedom, have lost their femininity, their modesty, their wamth, their shy grace.(他们认为，妇女赢得了自由，却失去了妇女的气质、妇女的端庄、妇女的温柔和妇女的羞怯美。) They knew his ignorance,his cruelty, his avanice, his appetites, his sins.(他们深知他的愚昧、他的凶残、他的贪婪、他的野心、他的罪恶。)

We have bigger houses but smaller families; more conveniences but less time; we have more degrees but less common sense; more knowledge but less judgement; more experts but more questions; more medicine but less wellness.(我们的住房大了，但家庭小了；我们的生活便利多了，但时间少了；我的学位多了，但常识少了；我们的知识多了，但判断少了；我们的专家多了，但问题也多了；我们的药品多了，但健康少了。)作介词补足成分(即介词宾语): italy is famous for its food, music, art, and ancient architecture,and no one who have been there can fail to be impressed with Italy.(意大利以它的食品、它的音乐、它的艺术品、它的古建筑而闻名于世；凡到过那里的人没有不对意大利印象深刻。) A person with endeavouring spirit, creative thinking and a habit of hands-on leaming will eventually achieve success.(一个人只要有进取精神、创新思维和脚踏实地的读书习惯，终将事业有成。) They honor the pioneers for their faith in God, devotion in family, loyalty to church and county, hard work and service to others, courage in adversity, personal integrity, and unyielding determination.(人们尊敬这些先驱者，尊敬他们对上帝的信仰、对家庭的热爱、对教会和国家的忠诚、对他人努力工作和服务、在逆境中的勇气、正直的人格以及不屈不挠的决心。) As we both have fairly hectic work schedules, there is nothing better than coming home,relaxing on the balcony with a nice bottle of wine, some music and taking time out to breathe in the fresh ocean air.(由于我们两人的工作都很紧张，再也没有比回家更惬意的了：坐在阳台喝杯美酒，听听音乐，放松放松；有时还可走出去，呼吸海上的新鲜空气。)作补语(包括主语补语和宾语补语): Chaplin will keep you laughing for hours on end without effort. His fun is simple and sweet and spontaneous.(卓别林不费劲就能使你笑他几小时。他的笑貌是纯朴的、甜蜜的、自然的。) Mr Lorry found the valuable things safe, and strong, and sound, and stil, just as he had last seen them.(劳利先生发现那些宝物仍然安全、稳当、完好、平静地躺在原处，正如当初发现时那样。)作状语：

Dear Doctor Manette, I love your daughter fondly, dearly, disinterestedly, devoted.(Dickens)(亲爱的马耐特医生，我对你女儿的爱情是纯洁的、真诚的、无私的、全心全意的。) Read not to contradict and confute; nor to believe and take for granted; nor to find talk and discourse; but to weigh and consider.(Bacon)(阅读不是为了反驳，不是为了轻信，不是为了寻找谈话资料，而是为了明辨慎思。) For example, computer software will be designed to coach people about their health, to enable people to interact with medical care, even to help them plan meals, monitor their diets to make sure they are healthy and to assist them in making sure medicine is taken properly at the night time.(例如，计算机软件可用以指导保健、可帮人参与医疗、甚至可帮人准备饭菜和控制饮食以确保健康、还可帮人确认是否已经按时服药。) 3)排比的语义特征由上述诸例可以看出，在排比中，排比项的排列顺序在语义上通常是由轻到重，把说话人认为最重要的信息放在排比结构的末端，以达到加强语气的修辞效果。这在修辞上叫做“高潮”(Climax)。例如：

The letter, short, cold, sharptongued, was unexpected.(这封信，简短、冷漠、尖刻，真是出人意料。) I can't spare you any time. No,can't. Not a month,not a week,not even a single day.(我不可能为你花费时间。不，就是不可能!一个月不可能，一星期不可能，哪怕一天也不可能。) You can come for help any time;in the evening, after class, or even before bedtime.(任何时候，你都可以来求助，在晚上、在课后、甚至在就寝之前。) 358 第31讲

Einstein was a fair amateur violinist, a great mathematician, and a deeply philosophical man.(爱因斯坦是一位出色的业余小提琴手、一位伟大的数学家、一位有高深哲学思想的人。) This law should meet with your approval because it is simple,because it is enforceable, and because it is just.(这条法律你们应该批准，因为它简单明了、切实可行、公平合理。)在汉语中，排比项的排列顺序也有类似的情况。例如：

“⋯⋯人固有一死，或重于泰山，或轻于鸿毛，用之所趋异也。太上不辱先，其次不辱身，其次不辱理色，其次不辱辞令，其次诎体受辱，其次易服受辱，其次关木索、被篓楚受辱，其次剔毛发、婴金铁受辱，其次毁肌肤、断肢体受辱，最下腐刑极矣。”(司马迁《报任安书》)正因为排比结构的末尾通常都是最重要的信息，如果作者反其道而行之，故意出人意料，把一个按常理不太重要的信息放在最受瞩目的位置上就会达到讽刺、幽默的修辞效果。例如：

He owes his success to his hard work, his intellect, his perseverance, and his father-in-law.(他事业有成是由于他埋头苦干，他智慧不凡，他坚忍不拔，以及他的岳父大人。) The long climb to the summit rewarded us with a spectacular view,invigorating ai, and sore feet.(我们努力攀登，到达山顶，那里景色秀丽、空气新鲜、脚也起泡了。) 4)排比在语篇中的应用如前所述，排比是一种增强语势的修辞手法，常用于正式的、表达强烈感情的场合，比如丘吉尔在二战存亡之秋，号召英国人民坚决抗击法西斯侵略的演说中说：

⋯. We shall go on to the end, we shall fight in France, we shall fight on the seas and oceans, we shall fight with growing confidence and growing strength in the air,we shall defend our island, whatever the cost may be, we shall fight on the beaches, we shall ight on the landing grounds, we shallfight in the fields and in the streets, we shall fight in the hills... (一Churchill)又例如马丁·路德·金号召美国黑人反对种族歧视的演说中说：

... With this faith we will be able to hew out of the mountain of despair a stone of hope. With this faith we will be able to transform the jangling discords of our nation into a beautiful symphony of brotherhood. With this faith we will be able to work together, to pray together, to struggle together, to go to jail together, to stand up for freedom together, knowing that we will be free one day.又例如帕特尼·亨利在国会演说中说：

In vain, after these things, may we indulge the fond hope of peace and reconciliation. There is no longer any hope for peace.If we wish to be free—if we mean to preserve inviolate those inestimable privileges for which we have been so long contending—if we mean not basely to abandon the noble struggle in which we have been so long engaged, and which we have pledged ourselves never to abandon, until the glorious object ofour contest shall be obtained一we must fight! I repeat it, sir, we must fight! An appeal to arms and to the God of Hosts is all that is left us.排比结构也常用于诙谐幽默的语篇中。下面引用的是英国作家A.G.Gardiner所著幽默小品A Fellow-traveller(旅伴)中的一个段落：

There is a pleasant sense of freedom about being free in a carriage that is jolting noisily through the night. It is liberty and unrestraint in a very agreeable form. You can do anything you like. You can talk to yourself as loud as you please and no one will hear you.You can have that argument out with Jones and roll him triumphantly in the dust without fear of a counterstroke. You can stand on your head and no one will see you.You can sing, or dance a two-step, or practise a golf stroke, or play marbles on the floor without let or hindrance.You can open the

window or shut it without provoking a protest.You can open both windows or shut both. Indeed, you can go on opening them or shutting them as a sort of festival of freedom.You can have any corner you choose and try all of them in tum. You can lie at full length on the cushion and enjoy the luxury of breaking the regulations and possibly the heart of D.O.R. A.herself. Only D.O.R. A.will not know that her heart is broken.You have escaped even D.O.R.A. (注：D.O.R.A.(Defence of the Realm Act)指第一次世界大战期间英国的“本土保卫法”。)

### EXERCISE 31D

Translate the following into Chinese, noting the treatment of the coordinate/parallel constructions 1. She stole credit cards from her victims, withdrew cash from their accounts and splurged thousands more on luxury goods before she was eventually caught in New South Wales, where she was jailed for four years. 2. Here,he is no match for a street light,no match for an imitation of an ancient vase in the shop window, no match for the purple skirt sweeping by,no match for the blue-circled eyes and blood-red lipped decorations,and still no match for the little dog pulled by the hand ofa lady. 3. The miracles of modern medicine, the superior advancement of technology and the drastic alternation of employment and job descriptions certainly blend together to provide an explanation for greater life longevity,behavioral patterns and state of mind. 4. The scenic park with a wide lake spotted with boats moving on the rippled surface and a quiet tree-lined winding road was, is and likely always will be a place to go on a date. 5.The authorities must enact tougher regulations, including limiting adolescents' access to online games, requiring schools to teach classes on the effects of excessively playing online games and implementing regulations giving game players fewer points if they spend more than a few hours at a stretch in front of their PCs. 6. Some books are to be tasted, others to be swallowed,and some few to be chewed and digested. 7. The true enemies of science,argues Paul Ehrllch of Stanford University, a pioneer of environment studies,are those who question the evidence supporting global warming, the depletion of the ozone layer and other consequences of industrial growth. 8. In order to resolve these problems, the Chinese and American experts agreed that youth organizations should call on the whole society to create favorable conditions for the healthy growth of young people, and encourage them to meet the urgent needs of society and to challenge the prejudice that young people are apathetic and uncaring. 9. Unsuccessful job hunters should focus on where their problems first occur:if they can’t locate job openings, they may need to learn networking skills, if they apply for positions but not invited to interview,the problem may be their resumes,and if they land interviews but no offers, their interview skills might need work. 10. While almost every department of the federal government is concerned about and involved with some aspects of education,and while federal government influence is an important factor in education, the regulation of education at all levels remains a responsibility of the states. 360第31讲

11.Yale, known for its emphasis on solid foundation,regarded as the cradle of American presidents and pursued by many talented students both home and abroad,has made another breakthrough recently in the computer field, winning the worldwide admiration and recognition. 12. We have a place,all of us,in a long story—a story we continue,but whose end we will not see, a story of a new world that became a friend and liberator of the old, a story of a slave-holding society that became a servant of freedom, a story of a power that went into the world to protect but not possess, to defend but not to conquer.

# 第32讲 从属结构

## 32.1 从属结构和复杂句

1)什么是从属结构按照层次性理念，英语的语法结构通常是由紧邻的下一层次的结构组成。如果一个语法结构含有同一层次甚至高一层次的结构为其直接成分，这种现象叫做“从属”(Subordination)。例如 The man with a gun in his hand; the man who was holding agun in his hand。从属于其他语法结构的结构叫做“从属结构”(Subordinate Construction)。

从属结构可以是词组从属于单词：

Shakespear the great dramatist词组从属于词组：

a person ofendeavouring spirit分句从属于词组：

the house where Chairman Mao once lived分句从属于单词：

Tom,who lived next door to us分句从属于分句：

He is going to marry the old lady because he has to. 2)什么是复杂句从属分句分为形容词性分句(即关系分句)、名词性分句、副词性分句(即状语分句)。那带有名词性分句或状语分句作为自己直接成分的句子便是复杂句。例如：

That she should forget me so quickly was ratherashock. Take whatever you want. I slipped on the floor while/was having a shower. Ifyou need help, just let me know. She decided to buy the car although it was expensive. 362 第32讲

## 32.2 从属连词 —

从属分句通常带有从属连词。按照结构形式，从属连词分为简单从属连词(Simple Subordinator)、复杂从属连词(Complex Subordinator)、关联从属连词(Correlative Subordinator)和边际从属连词(Marginal Subordinator)。分述如下。

1)简单从属连词简单从属连词即单词从属连词(One-word Subordinator),如 after, although, as, because, before, directly,for,if,immediately,lest, like, once, since, that, though,till, unless, until, whatever,when(ever), where(ver),whereupon, whichever,while, whilst等。例如：

I went home directly(=as soon as)I had finished work. Until she spoke,I hadn't realized she was a foreigner. Once you understand this rule,you'll haven further difficulty. You can stay to dinner if you like. 2)复杂从属连词由两个或两个以上单词构成的从属连词叫做复杂从属连词。这类连词有的是以that 结尾，如assuming(that),but that, considering (that),except(that),excepting(that),for all(that),given (that),granted (that),granting(that),in that, in order that, insofar that, in the event that,now (that),provided (that),save that, seeing(that),so(that),such that,supposing(that)等。

还有一些复杂从属连词是以as 结尾的，如according as,as far as, as long as, as soon as, forasmuch as, inasmuch as, insofar as, insomuch as等。

此外还有as if, as though, in case等复杂从属连词。例如：

Now that we are alone, we can talk freely. David would do the work provided(that)you would pay him. It gave him such a shock that his face tumed pale. His heart beat fast so that he could hardly breathe. Seeing (that)he is lawfully old enough to join the army, I don't know why you should stop him. Granted (that) he is not brilliant, he is at least competent. 3)关联从属连词由两个关联词构成的从属连词叫做关联从属连词，如as ... as, as. ... so, barely / hardly / scarcely.⋯ when, more/(-er)/ less ... than, no sooner ... than,not so... as, so ... that,such ...as, such ... that, the... the, whether...or等。例如：

i don'tknow whether he is telling the truth or not. We had no sooner set out than a thunderstorm broke. Such advice as he was given proved almost worthless. The more she thought about it, the more depressed she became. When she heard the news, she was more frightened than encouraged. 4)边际从属连词边际从属连词指某些实际起着从属连词作用的随意结构，如even if, if only,just as, only if, every time (that),the instant(that),the moment (that),due to the fact that, for the reason that, by reason that, for fear(that),in spite of the fact that, in the light of the fact that, on account of the fact that, on the ground(s)that, regardless of the fact that, in the sense that等。例如：

He felt nervous each time the boss spoke to him. Old Tom died the moment his son arrived home. We spoke in whispers for fear that we might wake the baby.

He continued speaking regardless of the fact that he was hurting me. 5)若干语义相关的从属连词之用法比较在以上4类从属连词中，有些是语义相关的，比如都可以引导时间状语或原因状语。本节将就以下两组连词的用法作一些比较。

a) when, while, as, before, after,until等的用法比较这一类从属连词都可引导时间状语，但在具体用法上，有些可以交替使用，有些又有所不同。本节将从表示“同时性”、“先时性”、“后时性”等语义意图方面，对它们的用法作一些比较说明。

第一，表示同时发生的事态两个事件的发生在时间上没有先后之分，或者说没有明显的先后之分，这就是我们在这里所讲的同时性(Same Time)。这有以下几种情况。

两个同时发生的事件，如果不是一次性而是重复性，即过去或现在的习惯动作，连词可用when, whenever。例如：

When he goes to town, he(always) visits his aunt. She(always) felt ill when she ate oyster.这里的when意为“每当⋯⋯的时候”,相当于whenever: Whenever he goes to town, he visits his aunt. She felt ill whenever she ate oyster.在上述两例中，不管是用when还是whenever,主句动词和时间状语分句动词或者都用一般现在时，或者都用一般过去时，分别表示现在或过去的习惯动作。when/whenever的上述用法可以和if交替使用。

例如：

If he goes to town,he brings us a present.=When/Whenever he goes to town.这里的if/when都表示whenever和every time的意思。

两个同时发生的事件，如果都有延续性，而且延续时间大致相等，连词可用 when,while。例如：

The wind blew hard when the rain poured down. While I read, she sang.在这里blew,poured, read, sang 都表示持续性动作，前后都用一般过去时，有时也可前后都用过去进行体。比较：

I was cooking the dinner while he was playing the piano.=I cooked the dinner while he played the piano.这两句意义基本相同，只是用过去进行体更加突出事件的持续性。若要强调两个事件的延续时间正好相等，可用as long as。例如：

He worked as long as we played. The children were happy as long as the game continued.两个同时发生的事件，如果都无延续性，连词可用when, as soon as, just as, the instant, the moment, directly,immediately等。例如：

The dog barked when it heard a noise. She informed her husband as soon as she arrived home. The instant i saw him,I knew he was my lost brother. She knew he wasn'tan Englishman the moment he began to speak English. I'l tell you directly he comes. Tell me immediately you have any news.当然也可以用hardly/scarcely ... when和no sooner...than等关联连词来强调两个非持续性事件的同时发生。例如：

I had hardly left when the quarrel started. 364 第32讲

The doctor had scarcely got into bed when he was called out again. We had no sooner sat down than we found it was time to go.上述三例均可用倒装词序：

Hardly had l left when the quarrel started. Scarcely had the doctor got into bed when he was called out again. No sooner had we sat down than we found it was time to go.如果甲事件发生在乙事件的过程之中，通常是乙事件有延续性，甲事件无延续性或者只有较短的延续性，这时，表示乙事件的动词可用进行体或非进行体，表示甲事件的动词用非进行体，连词可用 while, when, as。例如：

I broke a glass while I was cooking the dinner. The waiter spilt soup over me when he was serving/served me. I read a newspaper while I was waiting/waited. I met Mary as Iwas going/went home.有时也可用when引导无延续性事件的分句。例如：

I was cooking the dinner when I broke a glass. I was going home when 1 met Mary.不过，这时 when-分句不同于时间状语分句，而是相当于一个并列分句：

I was cooking the dinner,and at that time l broke a glass. I was going home,and at that time I met Mary.第二，表示先时和后时发生的事态先时性(Earlier Time)和后时性(Later Time)指两个连续发生的事件在时间顺序上有先后之分，这时就要用before,after, till /until, when, since等连词(或介词)来表示。例如：

Before he eats,he washes his hands. She looked both ways before she crossed the road. He watches television after he does his homework. After he painted the windows, he painted the doors.由before和after表示甲事件先于乙事件或者后于乙事件的用法，通常可以改用when表示。试比较：

Before he eats, he washes his hands.→When he has washed his hands, he eats. After he painted the windows, he painted the doors.→ When he had painted the windows, he painted the doors.由此可见，when, after, before 在表示先时性和后时性方面有着一定的语义联系，如果也把till / until考虑在内，有时相同的内容能用多种不同的方法表示。试观察下列诸例：

When I reached the station, the train had left. I reached the station after the train had left. The train had left before l reached the station. I didn't reach the station until (after) the train had left.由于before / after的词汇意义关系，在上述诸例中主句和 before-/ after-分句动词也可都用一般过去时，意义不变：

I reached the station after the train left. The train left before I reached the station.当when=after时，它所引导的时间状语分句有时也可用一般过去时替代过去完成体：

When the teacher(had) left the room, the children started talking. b) because,for, since,as, now that等的用法比较表示原因，可用because,for, since,as, now that, seeing that等从属连词。如前所述，for是介乎并列连词与从属连词之间的，现在我们把它放在从属连词中与because作比较说明。

说明一，because与for都可引导原因状语分句，既可表示“直接理由”,即对某一情况说明原因：

We humied because/for it was getting dark.也可表示“间接理由”,即对说话人为何持此看法说明理由：

It must be very late, because/for the streets are quite deserted.在这里，because-/ for-分句并不是为“天已很晚”这一事实说明原因，而是为说话人为何作此推断提供理由，相当于：

It must be very late, and I claim it because/for the streets are quite deserted.言下之意，我之所以作此推断，是因为街上已经没有行人了。从以上意义来看，连词for与because的用法基本相同，从而接近于从属连词，只是because比for 语势较强罢了。在当代英语中，凡是for与because 可以互换的场合，通常以用because为多，因为for-分句常见于正式语体，在非正式语体中(特别是口语中)for-分句往往带有书卷气。

另一方面，because与for的用法也有不同之处。第一，作为原因状语，because-分句位置比较灵活，既可位于主句之前，也可位于其后。例如：

The doctor looks tired and sleepy because he sat up all night with the patient. Because he sat up all night with the patient, the doctor looks tired and sleepy.在这里，如果用for-分句，其位置便比较固定，只可位于主句之后：

The doctor looks tired and sleepy, for he sat up all night with the patient.第二，由because引导的原因状语分句可单独用来回答why-问句，而for-分句不可以。例如：

—Why does the doctor look so tired and sleepy?一Because he sat up all night with the patient.第三，作为原因状语，because-分句可在because 之前带否定词或其他修饰语，还可带某些并列连词，而for-分句不可以。例如：

The doctor looks tired and sleepy simply because he sat up all night with the patient. The doctor looks tired and sleepy not because he isn't feeling well,but because he sat up all night with the patient.第四，作为原因状语，because-分句可作分裂句中心成分，也可用以回答why-问句，而for-分句不可以。例如：

It is because he sat up all night with the patient that the doctor looks tired and sleepy. Why are you laughing?一Because you look so funny.但是，当because-clause和 for-clause 不是表示直接原因，仅是作补充说明，两者便可以换用，而且只能位于主句之后，在口语中有停顿，在书写中用逗号分隔：

The days are short,because/for it is now December. It was very late at night, because/for there was no traffic in the streets. When I saw her in the river, I was frightened,because/for the currents there were dangerous.说明二，在because, since,as这三个从属连词中，以because 语势最强，since次之，as.又次之。

because通常表示“新信息”,即表示一种未知的原因，而since /as 则表示已知原因。所以，如前所述，在回答 why-问句时要用because-分句，而不用for-分句，同样也不用since-/as-分句。作为分裂句中心成分，如前所述，也只能用because-分句，而不用 for-分句，同样也不用 since-/as-分句。如前所述，在because-分句之前可用否定词或其他修饰语，这种分句之前还可用某些并列连词，但for-分句不可以，同样地since-/ as-分句也不可以。关于这一点，我们再补充数例：

I went to the state university chiefly because the tuition was cheaper. She didn't want to go to Africa,probably because the weather is too hot. I'm going there tomorrow because I have to,not because I want to. I dislike those expensive hotels, not because I cannot afford their material comforts, but because want to live a quiet life. 366 第32讲

由于since-/ as-分句通常表示已知的原因，所以常常出现于句首，这又是它们与 for-分句用法上不同之处。例如：

As Chile is a long, narrow country, the temperature varies considerably from north to south. Since he had a certain talent for composition, his English master encouraged him to write little pieces for the college magazine.当然 since-/as-分句也可位于句尾，作为一种补充说明。例如：

'll have to ask someone else, since you can't answer this question. He saw her,as they were both getting off the bus at the same time. c)原因状语分句还可由in that, inasmuch as, now that, seeing that等复杂连词引导。例如：

I'm ina slightly awkward position in that he is not arriving until the 10th. This machine differs from the other one in that it is more powerful. Now that the new regulations have come into force,there are far fewer accidents on the roads. Now that John is poor and unfortunate, his friends have left him in the lurch(舍弃了他)。

We can't expect him to know the story seeing that he hasn't got the book. Seeing that you live next door to Mrs Blake, you ought to be able to recognize her. Double sessions(分两批上课)were instituted inasmuch as the school was crowded. lam ready to accept your proposal inasmuch as I believe it to be the best you can offer.在口语中，seeing that和now that中的 that可以省略。例如：

What are you going to do seeing(that) they won't agree? Now(that) I'm here,I'd better stay. seeing that的含义相当于in view of the fact that,有时可与 since/as/now that 互换使用。例如：

Seeing that/Since/As he left school as long ago as 1960,he must be over forty now. Now that/Seeing that/Since/As we are all here,the meeting can begin. now that具有原因和时间双重概念，既可用于现在时间也可用于过去时间表示同时性：

We are happy now that everybody is present. Now that she could drive, she felt independent.至于in that和inasmuch as,通常只用于正式语体。

d)原因状语分句还可由 on the ground(s) that, for the reason that, by reason that, for fear (that)等边际从属连词引导。例如：

He refused to disclose what had been told him, on the ground that it would be a breach of confidence. She is suing for divorce(提出离婚诉讼)on the grounds that he deserted her and the children. He cannot be charged for murder for the reason that he was not there when the crime was committed. We decided against installing new heating apparatus for the reason that it would be too costly. Wine is scarce here by reason that it is prohibited. We succeeded by reason that we are better organized. He is working hard for fear that he should fail. She didn't walk far for fear she would get lost.下面一则练习要求通过从属手段将两个简单句连接成为一个句子。

### EXERCISE 32A

Combine each pair of simple sentences into one sentence,using subordination. If more than one subordinator is available,make more than one sentence: 1. I saw a film yesterday. The film was dull. 2. The weather keeps fine. I see no reason why the village fete(游园会)should not be a success. 3. He didn't get up early enough.He missed the train. 4. A German is coming to see me.I made his acquaintance in Hamburg last year. 5. The students were trapped in a cave. One of them has already been brought out. 6. She spoke French rapidly. I couldn't understand her. 7. Any of these problems can be solved by ordinary intelligence and hard work. None of them are too difficult for us. 8. Illness kept him away from school.He didn't get the prize. 9. I lost my pen somewhere. The auditorium might be the place. 10. He ate too little and worked too hard for weeks on end. He became ill. 11. He came out of the Conference Room.Reporters surrounded him. 12. My wife was using the vacuum cleaner. I was trying to listen to a broadcast play. 13. You promise to behave yourself. You shall not go to the party this afternoon. 14. His mother waited up.He came home. 15. He had a sore throat. He had spoken for three hours.下面做一则练习，要求用适当的从属连词填空。
